- [ ] **Step 4: Write `cms/watchdog/policy.py`** (Tasks 9 to 11 add
  `decide()` and its rules to the end of this file):

```python
# (fork header)

"""The policy of cmsWatchdog: from observations to decisions (#38).

This module is pure. It imports only the standard library, knows
nothing about sockets, supervisord or PostgreSQL, and reads no clock:
every input is in an Observation and in the clocks given to decide(),
every output is a Decision, and what carries over from one cycle to the
next is in a PolicyState. Every threshold is a constant here, pinned
against the timeouts of the rest of CMS by
cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/ladder_test.py. The design is in
docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-watchdog-design.md.

"""

import json
import math
import time
from collections import deque
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field


# The cycle (spec 3.3). Seconds, unless the name says otherwise.
CYCLE_S = 15.0
# A cycle that starts this late is starved: it decides nothing.
STARVED_LAG_S = 5.0
# More than this since the previous cycle is a pause: every miss window
# is cleared.
PAUSE_S = 30.0

# The probes (3.4).
HTTP_TIMEOUT_S = 5.0
RPC_TIMEOUT_S = 3.0
RANKING_TIMEOUT_S = 5.0
GATE_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_S = 3
GATE_STATEMENT_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000
GATE_KILL_S = 7.0
LOG_ALERT_TIMEOUT_S = 2.0

# Liveness, R1 and R2 (3.5): misses among the last WINDOW_SIZE known
# results.
WINDOW_SIZE = 6
MISSES_TO_TRIP = 5
MISSES_TO_WARN = 3

# Sections, R3.
SECTION_TRIP_S = 180.0
SECTION_WARN_S = 90.0
ES_IDLE_TRIP_S = 60.0
ES_IDLE_WARN_S = 30.0
ES_CPU_LOOKBACK_S = 60.0
ES_CPU_MIN_S = 1.0
ES_CPU_KEEP_S = 90.0
ES_SECTION_PREFIX = "post_finish_lock:"
# The outermost decorated method of an invalidation (re-evaluation):
# EvaluationService._invalidate_submission_sync.
INVALIDATION_SECTION = "post_finish_lock:_invalidate_submission_sync"

# Workers, R4.
WORKER_DISABLED_TRIP_S = 180.0
WORKER_UNCONNECTED_TRIP_S = 300.0
ES_UP_FOR_WORKERS_S = 120.0

# Rankings: alert only.
RANKING_MISSES_TO_ALERT = 3

# FATAL programs, R5.
FATAL_GREEN_CYCLES = 2
FATAL_RED_LOOKBACK_S = 15.0

# Holds (3.6).
GREEN_BEFORE_RESTART_S = 120.0
LOCK_WAIT_S = 30.0
GLOBAL_FAILING_FRACTION = 1 / 3
GRACE_S = 120.0
BUDGET_WINDOW_S = 3600.0
PROGRAM_BUDGET = 2
POOL_BUDGET = 3
FATAL_PROGRAM_BUDGET = 2
CORRUPT_LEDGER_S = 3600.0

# Actions (3.7).
TERM_WAIT_S = 10.0
CONFIRM_S = 30.0
DID_IT_HELP_S = 120.0
USR1_WAIT_S = 1.0

# Incidents and alerts (3.5, 3.9).
PASSES_TO_RESOLVE = 2
ALERT_REPEAT_S = 300.0
SUMMARY_PERIOD_S = 3600.0

# The self-guard (3.11).
SELF_GUARD_S = 60.0

# The class of each managed kind (3.2): the kind is what follows "cms"
# in a program's command. Any other kind is never acted on.
KIND_CLASS: dict[str, str] = {
    "LogService": "infra",
    "ResourceService": "infra",
    "EvaluationService": "core",
    "ScoringService": "core",
    "ProxyService": "core",
    "ContestWebServer": "web",
    "AdminWebServer": "web",
    "Worker": "workers",
}
# The classes, in the order an R5 batch starts them.
CLASS_ORDER: tuple[str, ...] = ("infra", "core", "web", "workers")
# Judged on the favicon probe (R1).
HTTP_KINDS = frozenset({"ContestWebServer", "AdminWebServer"})
# Judged on health() (R2).
RPC_RULE_KINDS = frozenset({"LogService", "ResourceService",
                            "EvaluationService", "ScoringService",
                            "ProxyService"})
# Asked for health() every cycle: every asyncio service.
HEALTH_KINDS = HTTP_KINDS | RPC_RULE_KINDS
# The holds, in the order they are checked (3.6).
HOLDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("H1", "H2", "H3", "H4", "H5", "H6", "H7", "H8",
                          "H9")

# Decision.action and Booking.action.
ALERT = "alert"
RESTART = "restart"
START = "start"
# PolicyState.mode.
OBSERVE = "observe"
ENFORCE = "enforce"
# The budget pools of H7.
WORKERS_POOL = "workers"
SERVICES_POOL = "services"


def pool_of(klass: str) -> str:
    """Return the budget pool of a class: Workers have their own."""
    return WORKERS_POOL if klass == "workers" else SERVICES_POOL


def age(now: float, then: float) -> float:
    """Return now - then, or 0 if the clock went back (3.3)."""
    return max(0.0, now - then)


def hhmm_utc(wall: float) -> str:
    """Return an epoch time as "HH:MM UTC"."""
    return time.strftime("%H:%M UTC", time.gmtime(wall))


def p99(values: Sequence[float]) -> float | None:
    """Return the 99th percentile of values (nearest rank), or None."""
    if not values:
        return None
    ordered = sorted(values)
    return ordered[max(0, math.ceil(0.99 * len(ordered)) - 1)]


def is_number(value: object) -> bool:
    """Tell whether value is a finite int or float (not a bool)."""
    return (isinstance(value, (int, float))
            and not isinstance(value, bool) and math.isfinite(value))


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProgramFacts:
    """What the process source says about one program in one cycle.

    name: its name in the process source (supervisord's program name,
        or its label for the /proc source).
    kind: what follows "cms" in its command, or None for a command that
        is not a CMS program.
    shard: the argument after that, or None.
    state: supervisord's state name (RUNNING, FATAL, STOPPED...), or
        ABSENT for a program the /proc source does not see any more.
    pid: its process id, or None when it is not running.
    start_wall: when it last started (epoch seconds), or None.

    """

    name: str
    kind: str | None
    shard: int | None
    state: str
    pid: int | None
    start_wall: float | None

    @property
    def managed(self) -> bool:
        """Whether the watchdog may judge it and act on it."""
        return self.kind in KIND_CLASS and self.shard is not None

    @property
    def klass(self) -> str:
        """Its class; only for a managed program."""
        return KIND_CLASS[self.kind]

    @property
    def label(self) -> str:
        """Its CMS name, "cmsContestWebServer 2" (its application_name)."""
        return "cms%s %s" % (self.kind, self.shard)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Probe:
    """The result of one probe: ok, its latency, or why it failed."""

    ok: bool
    latency_s: float | None = None
    error: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Backend:
    """One row of the database snapshot (pg_stat_activity, 3.4.1).

    The ages are in seconds. A field PostgreSQL hides from another role
    (an operator's psql when the CMS role is not a superuser) is None.

    """

    pid: int
    application_name: str | None = None
    client_port: int | None = None
    state: str | None = None
    wait_event_type: str | None = None
    wait_event: str | None = None
    backend_age_s: float | None = None
    xact_age_s: float | None = None
    query_age_s: float | None = None
    state_age_s: float | None = None
    blocked_by: tuple[int, ...] = ()
    query: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Gate:
    """The database gate of one cycle: green with a snapshot, or red.

    reason: for a red gate, "refused", "timeout", "too many clients",
        "authentication" or "other".
    detail: the first line of psql's error, for the alert.

    """

    green: bool
    reason: str = ""
    detail: str = ""
    backends: tuple[Backend, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Observation:
    """Everything the probes saw in one cycle (3.3, step 2).

    lag_s: how late the cycle started.
    since_previous_s: the time since the previous cycle started, or
        None for the first cycle.
    supervisor_ok: whether supervisord answered; if not, programs are
        the last ones it gave.
    programs: every program of the process source.
    gate: the database gate.
    http: the favicon probe of each CWS and AWS, by program name.
    rpc: the health() probe of each asyncio service, by program name.
    health: each health() answer, by program name.
    workers: ES's workers_status() answer (by shard, as a string), or
        None when ES was not asked or did not answer.
    rankings: the probe of each ranking, by its visible address.
    holder_cpu: (native thread id, utime+stime in seconds) of the
        thread that holds ES's oldest section, or None.
    in_flight: whether an action of an earlier cycle is still running.

    """

    lag_s: float
    since_previous_s: float | None
    supervisor_ok: bool
    programs: tuple[ProgramFacts, ...]
    gate: Gate
    http: Mapping[str, Probe] = field(default_factory=dict)
    rpc: Mapping[str, Probe] = field(default_factory=dict)
    health: Mapping[str, Mapping] = field(default_factory=dict)
    workers: Mapping[str, Mapping] | None = None
    rankings: Mapping[str, Probe] = field(default_factory=dict)
    holder_cpu: tuple[int, float] | None = None
    in_flight: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Decision:
    """What a cycle decided: an alert line, a restart or a start batch.

    action: ALERT, RESTART or START.
    text: an alert's line; for an action, what tripped (the action's
        lines quote it).
    level: an alert's level: "INFO", "WARNING" or "ERROR".
    key: (program name or "-", topic): the alert's rate-limit key.
    program: the program to restart.
    programs: the programs of a start batch, in class order.
    rule: "R1" to "R5".
    would: a dry run (observe mode, or H9).
    shadow: booked in the shadow ledger (H9).
    holds: for a held trip's alert, the holds that apply, the headline
        first.

    """

    action: str
    text: str = ""
    level: str = "WARNING"
    key: tuple[str, str] = ("-", "")
    program: str = ""
    programs: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    rule: str = ""
    would: bool = False
    shadow: bool = False
    holds: tuple[str, ...] = ()


@dataclass
class Booking:
    """One action or would-action, kept for the budgets (H7).

    wall: when it was decided (epoch seconds).
    program: the program's name in the process source.
    label: its CMS name (the orphan mark of H3 looks it up).
    pool: WORKERS_POOL or SERVICES_POOL.
    action: RESTART or START.
    rule: the rule that tripped.
    would: whether it was a dry run.
    pid: the pid a restart signals, None for a start.

    """

    wall: float
    program: str
    label: str
    pool: str
    action: str
    rule: str
    would: bool
    pid: int | None = None


class LedgerError(ValueError):
    """A ledger file that does not parse."""


def _booking(item: Mapping) -> Booking:
    """Read one booking of a ledger file."""
    return Booking(
        wall=float(item["wall"]), program=str(item["program"]),
        label=str(item["label"]), pool=str(item["pool"]),
        action=str(item["action"]), rule=str(item["rule"]),
        would=bool(item["would"]),
        pid=None if item.get("pid") is None else int(item["pid"]))


@dataclass
class Ledger:
    """What must survive a restart of the watchdog (3.10).

    real: the actions, and the would-actions of observe mode.
    shadow: the would-actions of a class that enforce mode may not act
        on (H9).
    red: the red intervals of the gate, [start, end] in epoch seconds,
        end None for the current one.
    incidents: the open incidents by program name: {"rule", "opened"
        (epoch seconds), "passes"}.
    exhausted_until: until when a ledger that did not parse counts as
        every budget exhausted (epoch seconds), or None.

    """

    real: list[Booking] = field(default_factory=list)
    shadow: list[Booking] = field(default_factory=list)
    red: list[list] = field(default_factory=list)
    incidents: dict[str, dict] = field(default_factory=dict)
    exhausted_until: float | None = None

    @classmethod
    def exhausted(cls, wall: float) -> "Ledger":
        """Return the ledger that replaces one that did not parse.

        wall: now (epoch seconds).

        return: an empty ledger whose budgets are all exhausted for
            CORRUPT_LEDGER_S seconds.

        """
        return cls(exhausted_until=wall + CORRUPT_LEDGER_S)

    def book(self, booking: Booking, shadow: bool) -> None:
        """Add an action to the real ledger, or to the shadow one."""
        (self.shadow if shadow else self.real).append(booking)

    def prune(self, wall: float) -> None:
        """Drop what no budget and no R5 question can need any more."""
        def recent(then: float) -> bool:
            return age(wall, then) < BUDGET_WINDOW_S

        self.real = [b for b in self.real if recent(b.wall)]
        self.shadow = [b for b in self.shadow if recent(b.wall)]
        self.red = [interval for interval in self.red
                    if interval[1] is None or recent(interval[1])]
        if self.exhausted_until is not None and \
                wall >= self.exhausted_until:
            self.exhausted_until = None

    def count(self, program: str, action: str, shadow: bool,
              wall: float) -> int:
        """Return how many times action hit program in the last hour.

        program: the program's name.
        action: RESTART or START.
        shadow: whether to count in the shadow ledger.
        wall: now (epoch seconds).

        return: the count.

        """
        return sum(1 for b in (self.shadow if shadow else self.real)
                   if b.program == program and b.action == action
                   and age(wall, b.wall) < BUDGET_WINDOW_S)

    def free_at(self, program: str, pool: str, action: str, shadow: bool,
                wall: float) -> float | None:
        """Tell when the budget allows action on program again (H7).

        program: the program's name.
        pool: its pool (pool_of its class).
        action: RESTART (PROGRAM_BUDGET per program, POOL_BUDGET per
            pool) or START (FATAL_PROGRAM_BUDGET per program).
        shadow: whether to count in the shadow ledger.
        wall: now (epoch seconds).

        return: None if the budget allows it now, otherwise the epoch
            time from which it does.

        """
        if self.exhausted_until is not None and wall < self.exhausted_until:
            return self.exhausted_until
        recent = [b for b in (self.shadow if shadow else self.real)
                  if b.action == action
                  and age(wall, b.wall) < BUDGET_WINDOW_S]
        if action == RESTART:
            limits = (([b for b in recent if b.program == program],
                       PROGRAM_BUDGET),
                      ([b for b in recent if b.pool == pool], POOL_BUDGET))
        else:
            limits = (([b for b in recent if b.program == program],
                       FATAL_PROGRAM_BUDGET),)
        free = None
        for bookings, limit in limits:
            if len(bookings) >= limit:
                walls = sorted(b.wall for b in bookings)
                at = walls[len(walls) - limit] + BUDGET_WINDOW_S
                free = at if free is None else max(free, at)
        return free

    def red_since(self, wall: float) -> bool:
        """Tell whether the gate was red at some time after wall."""
        return any(end is None or end > wall for _start, end in self.red)

    def last_restart_of(self, label: str) -> Booking | None:
        """Return the last real restart of the program called label."""
        restarts = [b for b in self.real if b.label == label
                    and b.action == RESTART and not b.would]
        return restarts[-1] if restarts else None

    def to_json(self) -> str:
        """Return the ledger as the text of its file."""
        return json.dumps({
            "real": [asdict(b) for b in self.real],
            "shadow": [asdict(b) for b in self.shadow],
            "red": self.red,
            "incidents": self.incidents,
            "exhausted_until": self.exhausted_until,
        }, indent=1, sort_keys=True)

    @classmethod
    def from_json(cls, text: str) -> "Ledger":
        """Read the text of a ledger file.

        text: what to_json wrote.

        return: the ledger.

        raise (LedgerError): if text is not a ledger.

        """
        try:
            data = json.loads(text)
            return cls(
                real=[_booking(item) for item in data["real"]],
                shadow=[_booking(item) for item in data["shadow"]],
                red=[[float(start), None if end is None else float(end)]
                     for start, end in data["red"]],
                incidents={
                    str(name): {"rule": str(incident["rule"]),
                                "opened": float(incident["opened"]),
                                "passes": int(incident["passes"])}
                    for name, incident in data["incidents"].items()},
                exhausted_until=(None if data["exhausted_until"] is None
                                 else float(data["exhausted_until"])))
        except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, AttributeError) as error:
            raise LedgerError("not a watchdog ledger: %s" % error) \
                from error


@dataclass
class ProgramState:
    """What the policy remembers about one program.

    pid: its pid in the last cycle that saw it RUNNING.
    window: its last WINDOW_SIZE known liveness results, True for a
        miss.
    history: its last WINDOW_SIZE observations, for the evidence.
    last_action_mono: when its last action or would-action was decided
        (the grace of H6).
    restarted_mono, restarted_pid: when the watchdog last restarted it
        for real and the pid it had then, until the "did it help" line.

    """

    pid: int | None = None
    window: deque = field(
        default_factory=lambda: deque(maxlen=WINDOW_SIZE))
    history: deque = field(
        default_factory=lambda: deque(maxlen=WINDOW_SIZE))
    last_action_mono: float | None = None
    restarted_mono: float | None = None
    restarted_pid: int | None = None


@dataclass
class NearMiss:
    """How close each trip came in this hour (3.9).

    misses: the most misses in a window, by program label.
    latencies: the latencies of the passing liveness probes, by label.
    es_longest: (age_s, what) of the longest ES section.
    es_idle_s: the longest idle_s inside an ES section that is not an
        invalidation.
    es_half: every ES section that passed half its trip (not an
        invalidation), one entry per section: (what, its start as epoch
        seconds, its largest age_s, its largest idle_s), by a key made
        of its holder thread and its start. A section seen on many
        cycles stays one entry, so the summary grows with the number of
        such sections only, and lists every one of them.
    ss_longest: (age_s, execution key) of the longest SS execution.
    ss_overlapping: the keys of the SS executions that overlapped an
        invalidation.
    ps_idle_s: the longest idle_s inside a PS send.
    red_cycles, starved_cycles: how many cycles had a red gate, and
        how many were starved.

    """

    misses: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    latencies: dict[str, list[float]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    es_longest: tuple[float, str] | None = None
    es_idle_s: float = 0.0
    es_half: dict[str, tuple[str, float, float, float]] = field(
        default_factory=dict)
    ss_longest: tuple[float, str] | None = None
    ss_overlapping: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    ps_idle_s: float = 0.0
    red_cycles: int = 0
    starved_cycles: int = 0


@dataclass
class PolicyState:
    """What carries over from one cycle to the next.

    mode: OBSERVE or ENFORCE.
    restart_classes: the classes enforce mode may act on (H9).
    ledger: what survives a restart (main.py saves and loads it).
    programs: a ProgramState by program name.
    started_mono: when the watchdog's first cycle ran (H6).
    green_since_mono: when the gate last turned green; None while red.
    green_cycles: how many cycles in a row the gate has been green.
    holder_samples: (mono, tid, cpu_s) samples of ES's holder thread.
    ranking_misses: failed cycles in a row, by ranking address.
    alert_sent: when each alert key was last emitted (monotonic).
    held_headline: the headline hold of each held trip's key.
    open_alerts: the last alert of each open problem, by key (sent
        again to a new LogService).
    near_miss: this hour's statistics.
    summary_mono: when the last hourly summary was emitted.

    """

    mode: str
    restart_classes: frozenset[str]
    ledger: Ledger = field(default_factory=Ledger)
    programs: dict[str, ProgramState] = field(default_factory=dict)
    started_mono: float | None = None
    green_since_mono: float | None = None
    green_cycles: int = 0
    holder_samples: list[tuple[float, int, float]] = field(
        default_factory=list)
    ranking_misses: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    alert_sent: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    held_headline: dict[tuple[str, str], str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    open_alerts: dict[tuple[str, str], Decision] = field(
        default_factory=dict)
    near_miss: NearMiss = field(default_factory=NearMiss)
    summary_mono: float | None = None

    def shadow_for(self, klass: str) -> bool:
        """Tell whether an action on klass goes to the shadow (H9)."""
        return self.mode == ENFORCE and klass not in self.restart_classes
```

### Task 9: `decide()`: the liveness rules, the holds and the incidents

**Files:**
- Modify: `cms/watchdog/policy.py` (add `import copy`; append the code
  below at the end of the file)
- Create: `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/scenario.py` (a test helper,
  not collected: its name does not end in `_test.py`)
- Create: `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/policy_liveness_test.py`
  (asyncio group)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything Task 8 produced.
- Produces:
  - `decide(state: PolicyState, observation: Observation, mono: float,
    wall: float) -> tuple[PolicyState, list[Decision]]`; it never changes
    `state` (it works on a deep copy);
  - `Trip(program: ProgramFacts, rule: str, detail: str)`, `HOLD_HINTS`,
    `busy_sections(payload) -> list[dict]` (entries `{"what", "tid",
    "age_s", "idle_s", "statements"}`, oldest first; a malformed answer
    gives `[]`);
  - `_Cycle`, with the methods Tasks 10 and 11 call or extend:
    `run()`, `_alert(key, level, text, change=False, holds=(),
    keep_open=False)`, `_resolve_key(key, text)`,
    `_resolve_program(name, text)`, `_liveness_probe(program)`,
    `_holds(program, shadow)`, `_budget(program, action, shadow)`,
    `_lock_wait()`, `_grace(program)`, `_act_on_trips()`,
    `_restart(trip, shadow)`, and the attributes `state`, `obs`,
    `mono`, `wall`, `decisions`, `acted`, `starved`, `running`,
    `trips`, `failing`, `half`;
  - alert texts that contain `held by H<n>` for a held trip (the
    harness counts them), `WOULD` never (the would-action lines are
    `main.py`'s);
  - `scenario.py`: `Scenario(mode=ENFORCE, restart=ALL_CLASSES,
    programs=DEFAULT_PROGRAMS)` with `cycle(fail=(), *, gate=GREEN,
    lag_s=0.0, since=None, supervisor_ok=True, health=None, probes=None,
    workers=None, rankings=None, holder_cpu=None, in_flight=False,
    advance=CYCLE_S) -> list[Decision]`, `set(name, **changes)`,
    `warm_up()`, `observation` (the last cycle's); the names `LS`, `RS`, `SS`, `ES`, `PS`, `W0`, `W1`,
    `CWS0`, `CWS1`, `AWS`, `WALL0`, `MONO0`, `LONG_AGO`, `GREEN`, `RED`,
    `ALL_CLASSES`, `DEFAULT_PROGRAMS`, `program(...)`,
    `lock_wait_snapshot()`, `worker(connected, disabled_at=None)` (one
    entry of `workers_status()`; the later policy tests import it from
    here instead of copying it), `actions()`, `alerts()`, `held()`.
- A decision taken by this plan (also listed in the header): H6's "last
  would-action" grace also follows a real action. In enforce mode the
  restarted program's new start time already gives that grace; counting
  the action itself too keeps observe and enforce identical on the same
  observations (the invariant test of Task 11).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the scenario driver.** Create
  `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/scenario.py`:

```python
# (fork header)

"""Drive cmsWatchdog's policy cycle by cycle on a fake clock (#38).

A Scenario holds the programs and the policy state of a test. Each
cycle() builds the Observation the probes would give (every probe
passes unless its program is in fail), calls decide() and returns its
decisions.

"""

from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
from dataclasses import replace

from cms.watchdog.policy import ALERT, CYCLE_S, ENFORCE, HEALTH_KINDS, \
    HTTP_KINDS, Backend, Decision, Gate, Observation, PolicyState, \
    Probe, ProgramFacts, decide


WALL0 = 1_800_000_000.0
MONO0 = 10_000.0
LONG_AGO = WALL0 - 86_400.0
GREEN = Gate(True)
RED = Gate(False, "refused", "Connection refused")
ALL_CLASSES = ("web", "core", "infra", "workers")

LS = "cmslogservice"
RS = "cmsresourceservice"
SS = "cmsscoringservice"
ES = "cmsevaluationservice"
PS = "cmsproxyservice"
W0 = "cmsworker0"
W1 = "cmsworker1"
CWS0 = "cmscontestwebserver0"
CWS1 = "cmscontestwebserver1"
AWS = "cmsadminwebserver"


def program(name: str, kind: str | None, shard: int | None, pid: int,
            state: str = "RUNNING") -> ProgramFacts:
    """Return the facts of a program that started a day before WALL0."""
    return ProgramFacts(name, kind, shard, state,
                        pid if state == "RUNNING" else None, LONG_AGO)


DEFAULT_PROGRAMS: tuple[ProgramFacts, ...] = (
    program(LS, "LogService", 0, 101),
    program(RS, "ResourceService", 0, 102),
    program(SS, "ScoringService", 0, 103),
    program(ES, "EvaluationService", 0, 104),
    program(W0, "Worker", 0, 105),
    program(W1, "Worker", 1, 106),
    program(PS, "ProxyService", 0, 107),
    program(CWS0, "ContestWebServer", 0, 108),
    program(CWS1, "ContestWebServer", 1, 109),
    program(AWS, "AdminWebServer", 0, 110),
)


def lock_wait_snapshot(blocker_app: str = "psql",
                       blocker_age_s: float = 400.0
                       ) -> tuple[Backend, ...]:
    """Return a snapshot where ES has waited 45 s on another's lock."""
    return (
        Backend(pid=500, application_name="cmsEvaluationService 0",
                state="active", wait_event_type="Lock",
                wait_event="transactionid", query_age_s=45.0,
                backend_age_s=3_000.0, blocked_by=(900,)),
        Backend(pid=900, application_name=blocker_app,
                state="idle in transaction", xact_age_s=300.0,
                backend_age_s=blocker_age_s),
    )


def worker(connected: bool, disabled_at: float | None = None) -> dict:
    """Return one Worker's entry of ES's workers_status().

    disabled_at: when ES disabled it for not answering, or None.

    """
    return {"connected": connected,
            "disabled_by_timeout": disabled_at is not None,
            "disabled_at": disabled_at}


class Scenario:
    """The programs and the policy state of one test, cycle by cycle."""

    def __init__(self, mode: str = ENFORCE,
                 restart: Iterable[str] = ALL_CLASSES,
                 programs: Iterable[ProgramFacts] = DEFAULT_PROGRAMS):
        self.state = PolicyState(mode=mode,
                                 restart_classes=frozenset(restart))
        self.mono = MONO0
        self.wall = WALL0
        self.programs = {facts.name: facts for facts in programs}
        self.cycles = 0
        # The observation of the last cycle.
        self.observation: Observation | None = None

    def set(self, name: str, **changes) -> None:
        """Change the facts of a program from the next cycle on."""
        self.programs[name] = replace(self.programs[name], **changes)

    def cycle(self, fail: Iterable[str] = (), *, gate: Gate = GREEN,
              lag_s: float = 0.0, since: float | None = None,
              supervisor_ok: bool = True,
              health: Mapping[str, Mapping] | None = None,
              probes: Mapping[str, Probe] | None = None,
              workers: Mapping[str, Mapping] | None = None,
              rankings: Mapping[str, Probe] | None = None,
              holder_cpu: tuple[int, float] | None = None,
              in_flight: bool = False,
              advance: float = CYCLE_S) -> list[Decision]:
        """Run one cycle, advance seconds after the previous one.

        fail: the programs whose probes fail (HTTP and RPC).
        probes: probes to force, by program name (HTTP and RPC).
        health: health() answers to replace, by program name.
        workers: ES's workers_status(); by default every running Worker
            is connected, if ES's health() passes.
        since: the gap since the previous cycle; advance by default.
        The other arguments go to the Observation as they are.

        return: the decisions of the cycle.

        """
        if self.cycles:
            self.mono += advance
            self.wall += advance
        failing = set(fail)
        http: dict[str, Probe] = {}
        rpc: dict[str, Probe] = {}
        for facts in self.programs.values():
            if facts.state != "RUNNING" or facts.kind not in HEALTH_KINDS:
                continue
            probe = (Probe(False, None, "timed out")
                     if facts.name in failing else Probe(True, 0.01))
            if facts.kind in HTTP_KINDS:
                http[facts.name] = probe
            rpc[facts.name] = probe
        for name, probe in (probes or {}).items():
            http[name] = probe
            rpc[name] = probe
        answers: dict[str, Mapping] = {
            name: {"pid": self.programs[name].pid, "uptime_s": 9_999.0,
                   "busy": []}
            for name, probe in rpc.items() if probe.ok}
        answers.update(health or {})
        if workers is None and rpc.get(ES, Probe(False)).ok:
            workers = {
                str(facts.shard): worker(True)
                for facts in self.programs.values()
                if facts.kind == "Worker" and facts.state == "RUNNING"}
        if since is None and self.cycles:
            since = advance
        observation = Observation(
            lag_s=lag_s, since_previous_s=since,
            supervisor_ok=supervisor_ok,
            programs=tuple(self.programs.values()), gate=gate, http=http,
            rpc=rpc, health=answers, workers=workers,
            rankings=rankings or {}, holder_cpu=holder_cpu,
            in_flight=in_flight)
        self.observation = observation
        self.state, decisions = decide(self.state, observation, self.mono,
                                       self.wall)
        self.cycles += 1
        return decisions

    def warm_up(self) -> None:
        """Run 9 passing cycles: past the watchdog's and the gate's 120 s.

        The next cycle is 135 s after the first one.

        """
        for _ in range(9):
            self.cycle()


def actions(decisions: list[Decision]) -> list[Decision]:
    """Return the restarts and starts among decisions."""
    return [decision for decision in decisions if decision.action != ALERT]


def alerts(decisions: list[Decision]) -> list[Decision]:
    """Return the alerts among decisions."""
    return [decision for decision in decisions if decision.action == ALERT]


def held(decisions: list[Decision], name: str) -> list[Decision]:
    """Return the alerts about held trips of the program name."""
    return [decision for decision in alerts(decisions)
            if decision.key[0] == name and decision.holds]
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests.** Create
  `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/policy_liveness_test.py`:

```python
# (fork header)

"""Tests for cmsWatchdog's policy: liveness rules, holds, incidents."""

import unittest

from cms.watchdog.policy import ALERT_REPEAT_S, CYCLE_S, OBSERVE, \
    RESTART, SERVICES_POOL, Backend, Booking, Gate, Ledger, Probe, \
    hhmm_utc
from cmstestsuite.unit_tests.watchdog.scenario import CWS0, CWS1, \
    DEFAULT_PROGRAMS, ES, LS, RED, Scenario, actions, alerts, held, \
    lock_wait_snapshot, program


def book(s: Scenario, name: str, label: str, wall: float,
         pid: int = 4242) -> None:
    """Book a real restart of name in the scenario's ledger."""
    s.state.ledger.book(Booking(wall, name, label, SERVICES_POOL, RESTART,
                                "R1", False, pid), False)


def trip(s: Scenario, **kwargs) -> list:
    """Fail CWS1 five times; the last cycle gets kwargs."""
    for _ in range(4):
        s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
    return s.cycle(fail=[CWS1], **kwargs)


class TestLivenessRules(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_r1_trips_at_the_fifth_miss_and_not_before(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])), [])
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]))
        self.assertEqual(
            (restart.action, restart.program, restart.rule, restart.would,
             restart.shadow),
            (RESTART, CWS1, "R1", False, False))
        self.assertEqual(restart.text,
                         "5 of its last 6 favicon probes failed")

    def test_r2_trips_at_the_fifth_miss_and_not_before(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(fail=[ES])), [])
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(fail=[ES]))
        self.assertEqual((restart.program, restart.rule), (ES, "R2"))
        self.assertEqual(restart.text,
                         "5 of its last 6 health() calls failed")

    def test_the_sliding_window_trips_on_a_livelock(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for failing in ([CWS1], [CWS1], [], [CWS1], [CWS1]):
            self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(fail=failing)), [])
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]))
        self.assertEqual(restart.program, CWS1)

    def test_three_misses_give_an_early_warning(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        decisions = s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        [warning] = alerts(decisions)
        self.assertEqual(warning.key, (CWS1, "R1 warning"))
        self.assertIn("early warning: 3 of the last 6 favicon probes of "
                      "cmsContestWebServer 1 failed", warning.text)

    def test_starved_cycles_are_unknown_results(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            self.assertEqual(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1], lag_s=6.0), [])
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])), [])
        self.assertEqual(sum(s.state.programs[CWS1].window), 1)

    def test_a_starved_cycle_decides_nothing(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        self.assertEqual(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1], lag_s=6.0), [])
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]))
        self.assertEqual(restart.program, CWS1)

    def test_a_pause_clears_the_windows(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        decisions = s.cycle(fail=[CWS1], since=45.0, advance=45.0,
                            lag_s=30.0)
        [pause] = [d for d in decisions if d.key == ("-", "pause")]
        self.assertIn("paused for 45 s", pause.text)
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        self.assertEqual(list(s.state.programs[CWS1].window), [])
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])), [])

    def test_a_new_pid_clears_the_window(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        s.set(CWS1, pid=999)
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])), [])
        self.assertEqual(list(s.state.programs[CWS1].window), [True])

    def test_only_running_managed_programs_are_judged(self):
        s = Scenario(programs=DEFAULT_PROGRAMS + (
            program("cmstelegrambot", "TelegramBot", 0, 111),))
        s.warm_up()
        failing = Probe(False, None, "timed out")
        for _ in range(6):
            self.assertEqual(
                actions(s.cycle(probes={"cmstelegrambot": failing})), [])
        for state in ("STOPPED", "STARTING", "BACKOFF", "EXITED",
                      "UNKNOWN"):
            s.set(CWS1, state=state, pid=None)
            for _ in range(6):
                self.assertEqual(
                    actions(s.cycle(probes={CWS1: failing})), [])


class TestHoldsAlone(unittest.TestCase):

    def assertHeldBy(self, decisions: list, code: str):
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        [alert] = held(decisions, CWS1)
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds[0], code)
        self.assertIn("held by %s" % code, alert.text)
        return alert

    def test_h1_supervisord_did_not_answer(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        alert = self.assertHeldBy(trip(s, supervisor_ok=False), "H1")
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H1",))

    def test_h2_the_gate_is_red(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        decisions = trip(s, gate=RED)
        alert = self.assertHeldBy(decisions, "H2")
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H2",))
        self.assertIn("the database gate is red (refused)", alert.text)
        [gate] = [d for d in alerts(decisions) if d.key == ("-", "gate")]
        self.assertEqual(gate.level, "ERROR")

    def test_h2_waits_for_120_s_of_green_and_clears_the_windows(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        trip(s, gate=RED)
        decisions = s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        self.assertTrue(any(
            d.text.startswith("Resolved: the database gate is green again")
            for d in decisions))
        self.assertEqual(list(s.state.programs[CWS1].window), [True])
        for _ in range(4):
            self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])), [])
        # It tripped again, held by H2 as during the outage: the same
        # hold gives no new line, but the open alert says why.
        alert = s.state.open_alerts[(CWS1, "R1")]
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds[0], "H2")
        self.assertIn("back for only 60 s", alert.text)
        for _ in range(3):
            self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])), [])
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]))
        self.assertEqual(restart.program, CWS1)

    def test_h3_a_lock_wait(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        decisions = trip(s, gate=Gate(True, backends=lock_wait_snapshot()))
        alert = self.assertHeldBy(decisions, "H3")
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H3",))
        self.assertIn(
            "'cmsEvaluationService 0' (pid 500) has waited 45 s on a lock "
            "held by pid 900 ('psql', idle in transaction, transaction "
            "open 300 s)", alert.text)
        self.assertIn("Ending a blocker is a human decision", alert.text)
        self.assertNotIn("orphan", alert.text)

    def test_h3_shows_hidden_fields_as_unknown(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        snapshot = (
            Backend(pid=500, application_name="cmsContestWebServer 0",
                    state="active", wait_event_type="Lock",
                    query_age_s=31.0, blocked_by=(901,)),
            Backend(pid=901))
        alert = self.assertHeldBy(
            trip(s, gate=Gate(True, backends=snapshot)), "H3")
        self.assertIn(
            "pid 901 ('unknown', unknown, transaction open unknown)",
            alert.text)

    def test_h3_ignores_a_short_wait_and_a_session_of_another_program(
            self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        snapshot = (
            Backend(pid=500, application_name="cmsEvaluationService 0",
                    wait_event_type="Lock", query_age_s=30.0,
                    blocked_by=(900,)),
            Backend(pid=501, application_name="psql",
                    wait_event_type="Lock", query_age_s=500.0,
                    blocked_by=(900,)))
        [restart] = actions(trip(s, gate=Gate(True, backends=snapshot)))
        self.assertEqual(restart.program, CWS1)

    def test_h4_two_programs_of_the_class_fail(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS0, CWS1])
        decisions = s.cycle(fail=[CWS0, CWS1])
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        for name in (CWS0, CWS1):
            [alert] = held(decisions, name)
            self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H4",))
            self.assertIn(
                "2 programs of class web fail together "
                "(cmsContestWebServer 0, cmsContestWebServer 1)",
                alert.text)

    def test_h4_does_not_count_a_program_over_its_budget(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for wall in (s.wall - 600, s.wall - 300):
            book(s, CWS0, "cmsContestWebServer 0", wall, 108)
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS0, CWS1])
        decisions = s.cycle(fail=[CWS0, CWS1])
        [restart] = actions(decisions)
        self.assertEqual(restart.program, CWS1)
        [alert] = held(decisions, CWS0)
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H7",))

    def test_h5_a_third_of_the_programs_fail(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS1, ES, LS])
        decisions = s.cycle(fail=[CWS1, ES, LS])
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        for name in (CWS1, ES, LS):
            [alert] = held(decisions, name)
            self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H5",))
            self.assertIn("3 of the 8 programs probed failed a probe",
                          alert.text)

    def test_h6_the_program_just_started(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        s.set(CWS1, start_wall=s.wall + CYCLE_S - 60)
        alert = self.assertHeldBy(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]), "H6")
        self.assertIn("cmsContestWebServer 1 started 60 s ago", alert.text)

    def test_h6_the_watchdog_just_started(self):
        s = Scenario()
        # The gate was green long before: only H6 holds.
        s.state.green_since_mono = s.mono - 1000
        alert = self.assertHeldBy(trip(s), "H6")
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H6",))
        self.assertIn("the watchdog itself started 60 s ago", alert.text)

    def test_h6_the_last_would_action(self):
        s = Scenario(mode=OBSERVE)
        s.warm_up()
        [would] = actions(trip(s))
        self.assertTrue(would.would)
        alert = self.assertHeldBy(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]), "H6")
        self.assertIn("its last action or would-action was 15 s ago",
                      alert.text)

    def test_h7_the_budget(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        first = s.wall - 600
        for wall in (first, s.wall - 300):
            book(s, CWS1, "cmsContestWebServer 1", wall, 109)
        alert = self.assertHeldBy(trip(s), "H7")
        self.assertIn("will not restart cmsContestWebServer 1 before %s"
                      % hhmm_utc(first + 3600), alert.text)

    def test_h7_a_ledger_that_did_not_parse(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        s.state.ledger = Ledger.exhausted(s.wall)
        alert = self.assertHeldBy(trip(s), "H7")
        self.assertIn("its ledger did not parse", alert.text)

    def test_h8_another_action_in_flight(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        self.assertHeldBy(trip(s, in_flight=True), "H8")

    def test_h9_a_class_enforce_may_not_act_on(self):
        s = Scenario(restart=("core", "infra"))
        s.warm_up()
        [would] = actions(trip(s))
        self.assertEqual((would.program, would.would, would.shadow),
                         (CWS1, True, True))
        self.assertEqual(s.state.ledger.real, [])
        self.assertEqual([b.program for b in s.state.ledger.shadow],
                         [CWS1])


class TestHoldOrder(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_a_lock_wait_heads_a_correlated_failure(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        gate = Gate(True, backends=lock_wait_snapshot())
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS0, CWS1], gate=gate)
        decisions = s.cycle(fail=[CWS0, CWS1], gate=gate)
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        [alert] = held(decisions, CWS1)
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H3", "H4"))
        self.assertIn("held by H3", alert.text)
        self.assertIn("pid 900 ('psql'", alert.text)
        self.assertIn("Also: H4.", alert.text)

    def test_the_blocker_follows_another_headline(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        decisions = trip(s, supervisor_ok=False,
                         gate=Gate(True, backends=lock_wait_snapshot()))
        [alert] = held(decisions, CWS1)
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H1", "H3"))
        self.assertIn(" H3: a database lock wait: ", alert.text)


class TestOrphanMark(unittest.TestCase):

    def blocker_text(self, backend_age_s: float) -> str:
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        # The watchdog restarted ES (pid 99); the new ES started 50 s
        # before the cycle that trips.
        book(s, ES, "cmsEvaluationService 0", s.wall - 10, 99)
        s.set(ES, pid=204, start_wall=s.wall + 5 * CYCLE_S - 50)
        snapshot = (
            Backend(pid=500, application_name="cmsContestWebServer 0",
                    state="active", wait_event_type="Lock",
                    query_age_s=40.0, blocked_by=(900,)),
            Backend(pid=900, application_name="cmsEvaluationService 0",
                    state="idle in transaction", xact_age_s=120.0,
                    backend_age_s=backend_age_s))
        [alert] = held(trip(s, gate=Gate(True, backends=snapshot)), CWS1)
        return alert.text

    def test_a_backend_older_than_the_new_process_is_an_orphan(self):
        self.assertIn("transaction open 120 s), orphan of pid 99",
                      self.blocker_text(100.0))

    def test_a_younger_backend_may_be_the_new_process_own(self):
        self.assertNotIn("orphan", self.blocker_text(10.0))


class TestBudgets(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_exhaustion_repeats_and_recovery(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        first = s.wall - 10
        book(s, CWS1, "cmsContestWebServer 1", first, 109)
        book(s, CWS1, "cmsContestWebServer 1", s.wall - 5, 109)
        self.assertEqual(held(trip(s), CWS1)[0].holds[0], "H7")
        # The held alert repeats every 5 minutes, not every cycle.
        emitted = [bool(held(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]), CWS1))
                   for _ in range(int(ALERT_REPEAT_S / CYCLE_S))]
        self.assertEqual(emitted, [False] * 19 + [True])
        # An hour after the first restart, the budget allows one again.
        decisions = s.cycle(fail=[CWS1], since=CYCLE_S,
                            advance=first + 3600 - s.wall)
        [restart] = actions(decisions)
        self.assertEqual(restart.program, CWS1)

    def test_the_shadow_ledger_has_the_same_limits(self):
        s = Scenario(restart=("core", "infra"))
        s.warm_up()
        trip(s)
        history = [s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]) for _ in range(16)]
        self.assertEqual([i for i, d in enumerate(history) if actions(d)],
                         [7])
        [would] = actions(history[7])
        self.assertTrue(would.would and would.shadow)
        [alert] = held(history[15], CWS1)
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds[0], "H7")
        self.assertIn("(in the shadow ledger)", alert.text)
        self.assertEqual(s.state.ledger.real, [])
        self.assertEqual(s.state.ledger.count(CWS1, RESTART, True, s.wall),
                         2)

    def test_observe_mode_re_trips_after_each_grace(self):
        s = Scenario(mode=OBSERVE)
        s.warm_up()
        self.assertTrue(actions(trip(s))[0].would)
        history = [s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]) for _ in range(16)]
        self.assertEqual([i for i, d in enumerate(history) if actions(d)],
                         [7])
        self.assertEqual(held(history[15], CWS1)[0].holds[0], "H7")
        self.assertEqual(len(s.state.ledger.real), 2)


class TestIncidents(unittest.TestCase):

    def restarted(self) -> Scenario:
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        self.assertEqual(actions(trip(s))[0].program, CWS1)
        self.assertIn(CWS1, s.state.ledger.incidents)
        # The restart worked: a new pid.
        s.set(CWS1, pid=999, start_wall=s.wall + CYCLE_S)
        return s

    @staticmethod
    def resolved(decisions: list) -> list:
        return [d for d in decisions if d.text.startswith("Resolved:")]

    def test_an_incident_closes_after_two_passes(self):
        s = self.restarted()
        self.assertEqual(self.resolved(s.cycle()), [])
        [line] = self.resolved(s.cycle())
        self.assertIn("cmsContestWebServer 1 passes its checks again",
                      line.text)
        self.assertNotIn(CWS1, s.state.ledger.incidents)

    def test_a_failure_resets_the_passes(self):
        s = self.restarted()
        for failing in ([], [CWS1], []):
            self.assertEqual(self.resolved(s.cycle(fail=failing)), [])
        self.assertEqual(len(self.resolved(s.cycle())), 1)


class TestClocks(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_a_wall_clock_step_back_gives_ages_of_zero(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        s.set(CWS1, start_wall=s.wall + 10_000)
        [alert] = held(trip(s), CWS1)
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H6",))
        self.assertIn("cmsContestWebServer 1 started 0 s ago", alert.text)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add `import copy` to the imports of
  `cms/watchdog/policy.py`, and append this code at the end of the

```python


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Trip:
    """A rule that tripped for a program in this cycle."""

    program: ProgramFacts
    rule: str
    detail: str


# The next step that an alert names for each headline hold (3.9).
HOLD_HINTS: dict[str, str] = {
    "H1": "Check ./status.sh.",
    "H2": ("Nothing to do: the watchdog waits for the database (see "
           "\"When the database misbehaves\")."),
    "H3": ("End the blocker by hand only if it is stuck (see \"When the "
           "watchdog alerts\")."),
    "H4": ("Find what the failing programs share before restarting any "
           "of them by hand."),
    "H5": "Check the load of the server and of the database.",
    "H6": "Nothing to do yet: it is still starting.",
    "H7": "Restart it by hand if needed, after cmsWatchdog --snapshot.",
    "H8": "Nothing to do: another action is running.",
}


def busy_sections(payload: object) -> list[dict]:
    """Return the busy sections of a health() answer, oldest first.

    A malformed answer, or a malformed entry of it, counts as no
    section: no answer is trusted to be well formed.

    payload: a health() answer, or None.

    return: [{"what", "tid", "age_s", "idle_s", "statements"}]; tid and
        statements are None when the answer lacks them.

    """
    if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
        return []
    busy = payload.get("busy")
    if not isinstance(busy, list):
        return []
    sections = []
    for entry in busy:
        if not isinstance(entry, Mapping):
            continue
        what = entry.get("what")
        age_s = entry.get("age_s")
        idle_s = entry.get("idle_s")
        if not isinstance(what, str) or not is_number(age_s) \
                or not is_number(idle_s):
            continue
        tid = entry.get("tid")
        statements = entry.get("statements")
        sections.append({
            "what": what,
            "tid": tid if isinstance(tid, int) and is_number(tid) else None,
            "age_s": float(age_s),
            "idle_s": float(idle_s),
            "statements": statements if is_number(statements) else None,
        })
    sections.sort(key=lambda section: -section["age_s"])
    return sections


def decide(state: PolicyState, observation: Observation, mono: float,
           wall: float) -> tuple[PolicyState, list[Decision]]:
    """Turn one cycle's observation into decisions (3.3, step 3).

    state: what the previous cycle left; it is not changed.
    observation: what the probes saw in this cycle.
    mono: the monotonic time of the cycle's start.
    wall: the epoch time of the cycle's start.

    return: the new state, and the decisions in order: the alerts to
        emit now (already rate-limited), and at most one action (a
        restart, or the R5 start batch with its H9 part).

    """
    cycle = _Cycle(copy.deepcopy(state), observation, mono, wall)
    cycle.run()
    return cycle.state, cycle.decisions


class _Cycle:
    """One call of decide(): the phases of a cycle, in order."""

    def __init__(self, state: PolicyState, observation: Observation,
                 mono: float, wall: float):
        self.state = state
        self.obs = observation
        self.mono = mono
        self.wall = wall
        self.decisions: list[Decision] = []
        # Whether this cycle decided an action (H8).
        self.acted = False
        self.starved = observation.lag_s > STARVED_LAG_S
        self.running = [program for program in observation.programs
                        if program.managed and program.state == "RUNNING"]
        self.trips: list[Trip] = []
        # Programs whose own probe failed in this cycle, or that a rule
        # without a probe tripped: they do not pass (incidents).
        self.failing: set[str] = set()
        # Programs failing for H4: MISSES_TO_WARN misses or more, or a
        # section past half its trip.
        self.half: set[str] = set()

    def run(self) -> None:
        """Run the phases of the cycle, in order."""
        state = self.state
        if state.started_mono is None:
            state.started_mono = self.mono
        if state.summary_mono is None:
            state.summary_mono = self.mono
        state.ledger.prune(self.wall)
        self._track_gate()
        self._track_pause()
        self._track_programs()
        if self.starved:
            state.near_miss.starved_cycles += 1
            return
        self._check_pids()
        self._liveness_trips()
        self._act_on_trips()
        self._update_incidents()

    # Alerts.

    def _alert(self, key: tuple[str, str], level: str, text: str,
               change: bool = False, holds: Sequence[str] = (),
               keep_open: bool = False) -> None:
        """Emit an alert, at most once per ALERT_REPEAT_S for its key.

        key: (program name or "-", topic).
        level: "INFO", "WARNING" or "ERROR".
        text: the line.
        change: whether it is a state change, emitted at once.
        holds: for a held trip, the holds that apply.
        keep_open: whether it stays open (and is sent again to a new
            LogService) until it is resolved.

        """
        decision = Decision(ALERT, text=text, level=level, key=key,
                            holds=tuple(holds))
        if keep_open:
            self.state.open_alerts[key] = decision
        last = self.state.alert_sent.get(key)
        if change or last is None or self.mono - last >= ALERT_REPEAT_S:
            self.state.alert_sent[key] = self.mono
            self.decisions.append(decision)

    def _resolve_key(self, key: tuple[str, str], text: str) -> None:
        """Close the alert key with a "Resolved:" line."""
        for table in (self.state.open_alerts, self.state.alert_sent,
                      self.state.held_headline):
            table.pop(key, None)
        self.decisions.append(Decision(ALERT, text=text, key=key))

    def _resolve_program(self, name: str, text: str) -> None:
        """Close every alert of a program with a "Resolved:" line."""
        for table in (self.state.open_alerts, self.state.alert_sent,
                      self.state.held_headline):
            for key in [key for key in table if key[0] == name]:
                del table[key]
        self.decisions.append(
            Decision(ALERT, text=text, key=(name, "resolved")))

    # Bookkeeping.

    def _track_gate(self) -> None:
        """Keep the red intervals, the green streak and their alerts."""
        state, gate = self.state, self.obs.gate
        ledger = state.ledger
        red_open = bool(ledger.red) and ledger.red[-1][1] is None
        key = ("-", "gate")
        if not gate.green:
            state.green_since_mono = None
            state.green_cycles = 0
            if not red_open:
                ledger.red.append([self.wall, None])
            state.near_miss.red_cycles += 1
            self._alert(
                key, "ERROR",
                "Watchdog: the database gate is red (%s: %s); the "
                "watchdog restarts nothing while PostgreSQL does not "
                "answer. See \"When the database misbehaves\"."
                % (gate.reason, gate.detail or "no detail"),
                change=not red_open, keep_open=True)
            return
        if red_open:
            started = ledger.red[-1][0]
            ledger.red[-1][1] = self.wall
            self._clear_windows()
            state.green_since_mono = self.mono
            state.green_cycles = 1
            self._resolve_key(
                key, "Resolved: the database gate is green again after "
                "%d s red; restarts wait %d s more."
                % (age(self.wall, started), GREEN_BEFORE_RESTART_S))
        elif state.green_since_mono is None:
            state.green_since_mono = self.mono
            state.green_cycles = 1
        else:
            state.green_cycles += 1

    def _track_pause(self) -> None:
        """Clear every window after a pause of the watchdog (3.3)."""
        gap = self.obs.since_previous_s
        if gap is not None and gap > PAUSE_S:
            self._clear_windows()
            self.decisions.append(Decision(
                ALERT, key=("-", "pause"),
                text="Watchdog: paused for %d s (a host, VM or container "
                     "pause); its miss windows were cleared." % gap))

    def _clear_windows(self) -> None:
        """Forget every known liveness result."""
        for program_state in self.state.programs.values():
            program_state.window.clear()

    def _track_programs(self) -> None:
        """Note each running program's pid, history and liveness."""
        for program in self.running:
            program_state = self.state.programs.setdefault(
                program.name, ProgramState())
            if program_state.pid is not None \
                    and program_state.pid != program.pid:
                program_state.window.clear()
            program_state.pid = program.pid
            program_state.history.append(self._history_entry(program))
            probe = self._liveness_probe(program)
            # A starved cycle's result is unknown: no miss, no pass.
            if probe is not None and not self.starved:
                program_state.window.append(not probe.ok)

    def _liveness_probe(self, program: ProgramFacts) -> Probe | None:
        """Return the probe that R1 or R2 judges program on, if any."""
        if program.kind in HTTP_KINDS:
            return self.obs.http.get(program.name)
        if program.kind in RPC_RULE_KINDS:
            return self.obs.rpc.get(program.name)
        return None

    def _history_entry(self, program: ProgramFacts) -> dict:
        """Describe what this cycle saw of program, for the evidence."""
        entry: dict = {"wall": self.wall, "pid": program.pid,
                       "starved": self.starved}
        for channel, probes in (("http", self.obs.http),
                                ("rpc", self.obs.rpc)):
            probe = probes.get(program.name)
            if probe is not None:
                entry[channel] = asdict(probe)
        sections = busy_sections(self.obs.health.get(program.name))
        if sections:
            entry["busy"] = sections
        return entry

    def _check_pids(self) -> None:
        """Alert when another process answers on a program's port."""
        for program in self.running:
            payload = self.obs.health.get(program.name)
            pid = (payload.get("pid") if isinstance(payload, Mapping)
                   else None)
            if isinstance(pid, int) and not isinstance(pid, bool) \
                    and program.pid is not None and pid != program.pid:
                self._alert(
                    (program.name, "pid"), "WARNING",
                    "Watchdog: pid %d answers on the RPC port of %s, "
                    "which supervisord started as pid %d; the watchdog "
                    "does nothing about it. Check ./status.sh and ps."
                    % (pid, program.label, program.pid))

    # R1 and R2.

    def _liveness_trips(self) -> None:
        """Apply R1 and R2, with their early warnings (3.5)."""
        for program in self.running:
            if program.kind in HTTP_KINDS:
                rule, what = "R1", "favicon probes"
            elif program.kind in RPC_RULE_KINDS:
                rule, what = "R2", "health() calls"
            else:
                continue
            probe = self._liveness_probe(program)
            if probe is not None and not probe.ok:
                self.failing.add(program.name)
            window = self.state.programs[program.name].window
            misses = sum(window)
            if misses >= MISSES_TO_WARN:
                self.half.add(program.name)
            if misses >= MISSES_TO_TRIP:
                self.trips.append(Trip(
                    program, rule, "%d of its last %d %s failed"
                    % (misses, len(window), what)))
            elif misses >= MISSES_TO_WARN:
                self._alert(
                    (program.name, rule + " warning"), "WARNING",
                    "Watchdog: early warning: %d of the last %d %s of %s "
                    "failed; it is restarted at %d."
                    % (misses, len(window), what, program.label,
                       MISSES_TO_TRIP))

    # Holds and actions.

    def _act_on_trips(self) -> None:
        """Turn each trip into an action, or into an alert if held."""
        held_workers: list[Trip] = []
        incidents = self.state.ledger.incidents
        for trip in self.trips:
            program = trip.program
            opened = program.name not in incidents
            if opened:
                incidents[program.name] = {
                    "rule": trip.rule, "opened": self.wall, "passes": 0}
            shadow = self.state.shadow_for(program.klass)
            holds = self._holds(program, shadow)
            if holds and holds[0][0] == "H4" and program.kind == "Worker":
                held_workers.append(trip)
            elif holds:
                self._held(trip, holds, opened)
            else:
                self._restart(trip, shadow)
        if held_workers:
            self._held_workers(held_workers)

    def _holds(self, program: ProgramFacts,
               shadow: bool) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
        """Return the holds that apply to a trip of program, in order.

        program: the program that tripped.
        shadow: whether its action goes to the shadow ledger (H9).

        return: (code, reason) of each hold that applies. H9 is not in
            it: it turns the action into a would-action instead.

        """
        obs, state = self.obs, self.state
        holds = []
        if not obs.supervisor_ok:
            holds.append(("H1", "supervisord did not answer in this cycle"))
        if not obs.gate.green:
            holds.append(("H2", "the database gate is red (%s)"
                          % obs.gate.reason))
        elif self.mono - state.green_since_mono < GREEN_BEFORE_RESTART_S:
            back = self.mono - state.green_since_mono
            holds.append(("H2", "the database has been back for only %d s; "
                          "restarts wait %d s"
                          % (back, GREEN_BEFORE_RESTART_S)))
        for code, reason in (
                ("H3", self._lock_wait()),
                ("H4", self._correlation(program)),
                ("H5", self._global_failure()),
                ("H6", self._grace(program)),
                ("H7", self._budget(program, RESTART, shadow))):
            if reason:
                holds.append((code, reason))
        if self.acted or obs.in_flight:
            holds.append(("H8", "another action is in flight"))
        return holds

    def _lock_wait(self) -> str:
        """Describe the lock waits of CMS backends (H3), or ""."""
        gate = self.obs.gate
        if not gate.green:
            return ""
        by_pid = {backend.pid: backend for backend in gate.backends}
        waits = []
        for backend in gate.backends:
            if not (backend.application_name or "").startswith("cms") \
                    or backend.wait_event_type != "Lock" \
                    or backend.query_age_s is None \
                    or backend.query_age_s <= LOCK_WAIT_S:
                continue
            blockers = "; ".join(
                self._blocker(by_pid.get(pid), pid)
                for pid in backend.blocked_by) \
                or "a session the snapshot does not show"
            waits.append(
                "'%s' (pid %d) has waited %d s on a lock held by %s"
                % (backend.application_name, backend.pid,
                   backend.query_age_s, blockers))
        if not waits:
            return ""
        return ("a database lock wait: %s. Ending a blocker is a human "
                "decision" % "; ".join(waits))

    def _blocker(self, backend: Backend | None, pid: int) -> str:
        """Describe one blocker of a lock wait (H3)."""
        if backend is None:
            return "pid %d (not in the snapshot)" % pid

        def shown(value: object, form: str = "%s") -> str:
            return "unknown" if value is None else form % value

        text = "pid %d ('%s', %s, transaction open %s)" % (
            pid, shown(backend.application_name), shown(backend.state),
            shown(backend.xact_age_s, "%d s"))
        orphan_of = self._orphan_of(backend)
        if orphan_of is not None:
            text += ", orphan of pid %d" % orphan_of
        return text

    def _orphan_of(self, backend: Backend) -> int | None:
        """Return the old pid if backend is an orphan of a restart (H3).

        It is one when its application_name is that of a program the
        watchdog restarted and it started before that program's new
        process: the new process has the same application_name, so a
        younger backend may be its own.

        backend: a blocker.

        return: the pid the watchdog restarted, or None.

        """
        if backend.application_name is None \
                or backend.backend_age_s is None:
            return None
        restart = self.state.ledger.last_restart_of(
            backend.application_name)
        program = next((p for p in self.obs.programs if p.managed
                        and p.label == backend.application_name), None)
        if restart is None or program is None \
                or program.start_wall is None:
            return None
        if self.wall - backend.backend_age_s < program.start_wall:
            return restart.pid
        return None

    def _correlation(self, program: ProgramFacts) -> str:
        """Describe a failure of program's whole class (H4), or ""."""
        if program.kind == "Worker":
            tripped = sorted({
                trip.program.label for trip in self.trips
                if trip.program.kind == "Worker"
                and not self._over_budget(trip.program)})
            if len(tripped) >= 2:
                return "%d Workers trip together (%s)" % (
                    len(tripped), ", ".join(tripped))
            return ""
        failing = sorted(
            other.label for other in self.running
            if other.kind != "Worker" and other.klass == program.klass
            and other.name in self.half and not self._over_budget(other))
        if len(failing) >= 2:
            return "%d programs of class %s fail together (%s)" % (
                len(failing), program.klass, ", ".join(failing))
        return ""

    def _over_budget(self, program: ProgramFacts) -> bool:
        """Tell whether program used its own restart budget (H4)."""
        return self.state.ledger.count(
            program.name, RESTART, self.state.shadow_for(program.klass),
            self.wall) >= PROGRAM_BUDGET

    def _global_failure(self) -> str:
        """Describe a failure of a third of the probed programs (H5)."""
        probed = [program for program in self.running
                  if program.name in self.obs.http
                  or program.name in self.obs.rpc]
        failed = [program for program in probed
                  if any(not probes[program.name].ok
                         for probes in (self.obs.http, self.obs.rpc)
                         if program.name in probes)]
        if probed and len(failed) > GLOBAL_FAILING_FRACTION * len(probed):
            return ("%d of the %d programs probed failed a probe in this "
                    "cycle" % (len(failed), len(probed)))
        return ""

    def _grace(self, program: ProgramFacts) -> str:
        """Describe why program is still in its grace (H6), or ""."""
        since_start = self.mono - self.state.started_mono
        if since_start < GRACE_S:
            return "the watchdog itself started %d s ago" % since_start
        if program.start_wall is not None:
            running_for = age(self.wall, program.start_wall)
            if running_for < GRACE_S:
                return "%s started %d s ago" % (program.label, running_for)
        program_state = self.state.programs.get(program.name)
        if program_state is not None \
                and program_state.last_action_mono is not None:
            since_action = self.mono - program_state.last_action_mono
            if since_action < GRACE_S:
                return ("its last action or would-action was %d s ago"
                        % since_action)
        return ""

    def _budget(self, program: ProgramFacts, action: str,
                shadow: bool) -> str:
        """Describe why the budget holds action on program (H7), or ""."""
        ledger = self.state.ledger
        free = ledger.free_at(program.name, pool_of(program.klass), action,
                              shadow, self.wall)
        if free is None:
            return ""
        if ledger.exhausted_until is not None \
                and self.wall < ledger.exhausted_until:
            why = "its ledger did not parse, so every budget counts as used"
        elif action == RESTART:
            why = ("at most %d restarts of a program, and %d of its pool, "
                   "in 60 minutes" % (PROGRAM_BUDGET, POOL_BUDGET))
        else:
            why = ("at most %d starts of a FATAL program in 60 minutes"
                   % FATAL_PROGRAM_BUDGET)
        return "will not %s %s before %s: %s%s" % (
            "restart" if action == RESTART else "start", program.label,
            hhmm_utc(free), why,
            " (in the shadow ledger)" if shadow else "")

    def _held(self, trip: Trip, holds: list[tuple[str, str]],
              opened: bool) -> None:
        """Alert about a held trip, headed by its first hold."""
        program = trip.program
        key = (program.name, trip.rule)
        headline, reason = holds[0]
        codes = [code for code, _ in holds]
        previous = self.state.held_headline.get(key)
        self.state.held_headline[key] = headline
        text = "Watchdog: %s fails %s (%s) but is held by %s: %s." % (
            program.label, trip.rule, trip.detail, headline, reason)
        if len(codes) > 1:
            text += " Also: %s." % ", ".join(codes[1:])
        lock_wait = dict(holds).get("H3")
        if lock_wait and headline != "H3":
            text += " H3: %s." % lock_wait
        text += " " + HOLD_HINTS[headline]
        self._alert(key, "WARNING", text,
                    change=opened or previous != headline, holds=codes,
                    keep_open=True)

    def _held_workers(self, trips: list[Trip]) -> None:
        """Alert once about the Workers that H4 holds together."""
        key = ("workers", "H4")
        previous = self.state.held_headline.get(key)
        self.state.held_headline[key] = "H4"
        labels = ", ".join(sorted(trip.program.label for trip in trips))
        self._alert(
            key, "WARNING",
            "Watchdog: %d Workers trip R4 together (%s) and are held by "
            "H4: a correlated failure is left to a human. %s"
            % (len(trips), labels, HOLD_HINTS["H4"]),
            change=previous != "H4", holds=("H4",))

    def _restart(self, trip: Trip, shadow: bool) -> None:
        """Decide a restart, or a would-restart, and book it."""
        program = trip.program
        would = self.state.mode == OBSERVE or shadow
        self.state.ledger.book(Booking(
            self.wall, program.name, program.label, pool_of(program.klass),
            RESTART, trip.rule, would, program.pid), shadow)
        self.acted = True
        program_state = self.state.programs[program.name]
        program_state.last_action_mono = self.mono
        if not would:
            program_state.restarted_mono = self.mono
            program_state.restarted_pid = program.pid
        key = (program.name, trip.rule)
        self.state.held_headline.pop(key, None)
        self.state.alert_sent[key] = self.mono
        self.decisions.append(Decision(
            RESTART, text=trip.detail, key=key, program=program.name,
            rule=trip.rule, would=would, shadow=shadow))

    # Incidents.

    def _update_incidents(self) -> None:
        """Count the passes of the open incidents; close them after 2."""
        tripped = {trip.program.name for trip in self.trips}
        by_name = {program.name: program for program in self.obs.programs}
        incidents = self.state.ledger.incidents
        for name, incident in list(incidents.items()):
            program = by_name.get(name)
            if program is None or program.state != "RUNNING":
                continue
            if name in tripped or name in self.failing:
                incident["passes"] = 0
                continue
            incident["passes"] += 1
            if incident["passes"] >= PASSES_TO_RESOLVE:
                del incidents[name]
                self._resolve_program(
                    name, "Resolved: %s passes its checks again "
                    "(incident of %s, opened at %s)." % (
                        program.label, incident["rule"],
                        hhmm_utc(incident["opened"])))
```

### Task 10: The section rule (R3) and the Worker rule (R4)

**Files:**
- Modify: `cms/watchdog/policy.py` (replace `_Cycle.run`; add methods to
  `_Cycle`; edit `PolicyState`, `_act_on_trips`, `_early_warnings`,
  `_outlook` and `_restart` as Task 9 left them)
- Modify: `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/scenario.py` (append
  `HOLDER` and `busy()`)
- Create: `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/policy_sections_test.py`
  (asyncio group)
- Modify: `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/policy_liveness_test.py`
  (its `TestWorkersEpisode` moves to `policy_sections_test.py`, on
  `Scenario`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `_Cycle`, `busy_sections`, `Trip`, `Scenario` and its helpers,
  `worker()` included (Task 9); the `busy` entries of Tasks 3 and 4; the
  `workers_status()` fields of Task 5. From Task 9 as reviewed (read
  `cms/watchdog/policy.py` before this task): `self.warnings` (the early
  warnings, emitted by `_early_warnings()` at the start of
  `_act_on_trips()`, so that H4 sees every program's misses), `_outlook`,
  `_other_holds`, `_held` (the rate-limit rules of a hold episode,
  `held_headline` and `held_announced`, `URGENT_HOLDS`),
  `_forget_episodes(name)`, `_held_workers` and `WORKERS_H4_KEY`, and
  the R1/R2 rule that trips only when the latest probe failed too.
  `green_since_mono` is `mono - GREEN_BEFORE_RESTART_S` after the
  watchdog's first green cycle: code that needs "how long has the gate
  been green" since the watchdog started reads `green_cycles`.
- Produces: `_Cycle._record_holder_cpu()`, `_section_trips()`,
  `_worker_trips()` (and their helpers), called by `run()` before
  `_act_on_trips()`: R3 adds to `self.half` and puts its early warnings
  in `self.warnings`, R4 puts its trips in `self.trips`; Task 11
  replaces `run()` again with its final form.
- Produces (Task 9 code, amended): every early warning, R3's included,
  says when its rule trips (`_trip_point`), what the watchdog does then
  in this mode, the holds that apply now, and the next step; a restart
  ends every hold episode of its program (`_forget_episodes`), whatever
  rule tripped; the Workers' H4 episode ends only after
  `PASSES_TO_RESOLVE` non-starved cycles in a row in which H4 applies
  to no Worker trip (`PolicyState.workers_h4_quiet`,
  `_track_workers_episode`).
- Produces (in `scenario.py`, for this task's and Task 11's tests):
  `HOLDER = 4321` and `busy(pid, what, age_s, idle_s) -> dict`, a
  `health()` answer with one busy section held by `HOLDER`.
- A decision taken by this plan (also listed in the header): R4 (b) does not
  apply to a Worker that ES disabled less than 180 s ago. ES's `quit`
  makes it disconnect at once, and (b) would otherwise restart it
  before (a)'s 180 s, which exist so that ES's own `quit` has ended
  first.

```python
# The native thread id that busy() gives as the holder of its section.
HOLDER = 4321


def busy(pid: int, what: str, age_s: float, idle_s: float) -> dict:
    """Return a health() answer with one busy section, held by HOLDER."""
    return {"pid": pid, "uptime_s": 9_999.0,
            "busy": [{"what": what, "tid": HOLDER, "age_s": age_s,
                      "statements": 3, "idle_s": idle_s, "stack": None}]}
```
