```python
# (fork header)

"""Tests for cmsWatchdog's policy: the section and Worker rules."""

import unittest

from cms.service.EvaluationService import EvaluationService
from cms.watchdog.policy import ALERT_REPEAT_S, CYCLE_S, \
    INVALIDATION_SECTION, OBSERVE, RESTART, SERVICES_POOL, Backend, \
    Booking, Gate, Probe
from cmstestsuite.unit_tests.watchdog.scenario import ES, GREEN, HOLDER, \
    PS, SS, W0, Scenario, actions, alerts, busy, held, \
    lock_wait_snapshot, worker


RANKINGS_OK = {"http://localhost:8890/": Probe(True, 0.01)}
RANKINGS_DOWN = {"http://localhost:8890/": Probe(False, None, "refused")}


def es_busy(age_s: float, idle_s: float = 70.0,
            what: str = "post_finish_lock:_write_results_sync") -> dict:
    return busy(104, what, age_s, idle_s)


class TestEvaluationServiceSection(unittest.TestCase):

    def run_es(self, s: Scenario, ages: list[float], idle_s: float = 70.0,
               cpus: list[float] | None = None,
               advances: list[float] | None = None,
               gate: Gate = Gate(True)) -> list[list]:
        """Run one cycle per age; return the decisions of each."""
        history = []
        for index, age_s in enumerate(ages):
            history.append(s.cycle(
                health={ES: es_busy(age_s, idle_s)},
                holder_cpu=(HOLDER, cpus[index] if cpus else 5.0),
                advance=advances[index] if advances else 15.0, gate=gate))
        return history

    def test_it_trips_past_180_s_and_not_before(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        history = self.run_es(s, [105, 120, 135, 150, 165, 180, 195])
        self.assertEqual([bool(actions(d)) for d in history],
                         [False] * 6 + [True])
        [restart] = actions(history[6])
        self.assertEqual((restart.program, restart.rule, restart.action),
                         (ES, "R3", RESTART))
        self.assertIn("post_finish_lock:_write_results_sync",
                      restart.text)

    def test_a_section_that_issues_statements_never_trips(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        history = self.run_es(s, [200 + 15 * i for i in range(30)],
                              idle_s=5.0)
        self.assertEqual([d for d in history if actions(d)], [])

    def test_a_holder_that_uses_the_cpu_never_trips(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        history = self.run_es(s, [200 + 15 * i for i in range(10)],
                              cpus=[5.0 + 0.3 * i for i in range(10)])
        self.assertEqual([d for d in history if actions(d)], [])

    def test_an_active_statement_holds_the_trip_off(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        running = Gate(True, backends=(Backend(
            pid=500, application_name="cmsEvaluationService 0",
            state="active"),))
        history = self.run_es(s, [200 + 15 * i for i in range(8)],
                              gate=running)
        self.assertEqual([d for d in history if actions(d)], [])

    def test_a_write_to_a_client_that_does_not_read_does_not(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        writing = Gate(True, backends=(Backend(
            pid=500, application_name="cmsEvaluationService 0",
            state="active", wait_event_type="Client",
            wait_event="ClientWrite"),))
        history = self.run_es(s, [200 + 15 * i for i in range(5)],
                              gate=writing)
        self.assertEqual([bool(actions(d)) for d in history],
                         [False] * 4 + [True])

    def test_a_lock_wait_trips_and_is_held_by_h3(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        history = self.run_es(s, [200 + 15 * i for i in range(5)],
                              gate=Gate(True, backends=lock_wait_snapshot()))
        self.assertEqual([d for d in history if actions(d)], [])
        [alert] = held(history[4], ES)
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds[0], "H3")
        self.assertIn("fails R3", alert.text)
        self.assertIn("pid 900 ('psql'", alert.text)

    def test_the_cpu_lookback_takes_the_newest_sample_60_s_old(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        # 2 s of CPU between the first two samples, none after: only
        # the newest sample that is 60 s old (the second) shows a holder
        # with no CPU. The first one is 59.9 s old at the fifth cycle.
        history = self.run_es(
            s, [200, 215, 230, 245, 260, 275],
            cpus=[5.0, 7.0, 7.0, 7.0, 7.0, 7.0],
            advances=[15.0, 14.9, 15.0, 15.0, 15.0, 15.2])
        self.assertEqual([bool(actions(d)) for d in history],
                         [False] * 5 + [True])

    def test_a_re_evaluation_only_alerts_every_5_minutes(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        lines = []
        for index in range(21):
            decisions = s.cycle(health={ES: es_busy(
                200 + 15 * index, 300.0, what=INVALIDATION_SECTION)},
                holder_cpu=(HOLDER, 5.0))
            self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
            lines.append([d.text for d in alerts(decisions)
                          if d.key == (ES, "re-evaluation")])
        self.assertIn("re-evaluation still running for 3 min",
                      lines[0][0])
        self.assertEqual([bool(line) for line in lines],
                         [True] + [False] * 19 + [True])

    def test_the_invalidation_section_is_the_es_method(self):
        self.assertEqual(
            INVALIDATION_SECTION, "post_finish_lock:"
            + EvaluationService._invalidate_submission_sync.__name__)

    def test_an_r3_restart_ends_every_hold_episode_of_the_program(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        # ES stops answering; its R2 trip is held by H1.
        for index in range(5):
            s.cycle(fail=[ES], supervisor_ok=index < 4,
                    holder_cpu=(HOLDER, 5.0))
        self.assertEqual(s.state.held_headline[(ES, "R2")], "H1")
        # It answers again, stuck in its locked section: R3 restarts it,
        # and the restart ends its R2 hold episode too.
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(health={ES: es_busy(400.0, 300.0)},
                                    holder_cpu=(HOLDER, 5.0)))
        self.assertEqual((restart.program, restart.rule), (ES, "R3"))
        self.assertEqual([key for key in s.state.held_headline
                          if key[0] == ES], [])


class TestScoringAndProxySections(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_a_section_warning_says_when_it_trips_and_what_to_do(self):
        s = Scenario(mode=OBSERVE)
        s.warm_up()
        [warning] = [d for d in s.cycle(health={
            SS: busy(103, "score", 100.0, 100.0)})
            if d.key == (SS, "R3 warning")]
        self.assertEqual(
            warning.text,
            "Watchdog: early warning: one scoring of cmsScoringService 0 "
            "has run for 100 s. Past 180 s, the watchdog only logs the "
            "restart it would do (observe mode). Check ./logs.sh for its "
            "log; cmsWatchdog --snapshot cmsscoringservice writes its "
            "stacks.")

    def test_ss_trips_past_180_s(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        warning = s.cycle(health={SS: busy(103, "score", 100.0, 100.0)})
        self.assertEqual([d.key for d in alerts(warning)],
                         [(SS, "R3 warning")])
        self.assertEqual(
            actions(s.cycle(health={SS: busy(103, "score", 180.0, 180)})),
            [])
        [restart] = actions(
            s.cycle(health={SS: busy(103, "score", 195.0, 195.0)}))
        self.assertEqual((restart.program, restart.rule), (SS, "R3"))

    def test_a_send_that_keeps_starting_requests_never_trips(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for index in range(20):
            decisions = s.cycle(
                health={PS: busy(107, "send", 1000.0 + 15 * index, 5.0)},
                rankings=RANKINGS_OK)
            self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])

    def test_a_send_idle_past_180_s_trips_while_the_rankings_answer(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(
            health={PS: busy(107, "send", 500.0, 180.0)},
            rankings=RANKINGS_OK)), [])
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(
            health={PS: busy(107, "send", 515.0, 195.0)},
            rankings=RANKINGS_DOWN)), [])
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(
            health={PS: busy(107, "send", 530.0, 210.0)},
            rankings=RANKINGS_OK))
        self.assertEqual((restart.program, restart.rule), (PS, "R3"))

    def test_h4_counts_a_section_past_half_its_trip_only(self):
        for idle_s, restarted in ((100.0, False), (5.0, True)):
            with self.subTest(idle_s=idle_s):
                s = Scenario()
                s.warm_up()
                for _ in range(4):
                    s.cycle(fail=[ES], rankings=RANKINGS_OK, health={
                        PS: busy(107, "send", 2000.0, idle_s)})
                decisions = s.cycle(fail=[ES], rankings=RANKINGS_OK,
                                    health={PS: busy(107, "send", 2000.0,
                                                     idle_s)})
                self.assertEqual(bool(actions(decisions)), restarted)
                if not restarted:
                    [alert] = held(decisions, ES)
                    self.assertEqual(alert.holds[0], "H4")

    def test_an_es_section_that_issues_statements_is_not_correlated(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[SS], health={ES: es_busy(1000.0, 5.0)})
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(fail=[SS],
                                    health={ES: es_busy(1000.0, 5.0)}))
        self.assertEqual(restart.program, SS)

    def test_a_malformed_health_payload_is_no_section(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for payload in ({}, {"busy": "x"}, {"busy": [3, None]},
                        {"busy": [{"what": 3, "age_s": 999,
                                   "idle_s": 999}]},
                        {"busy": [{"what": "post_finish_lock:x",
                                   "age_s": "999", "idle_s": 999}]},
                        {"pid": "104", "busy": [{"what": "score"}]}):
            with self.subTest(payload=payload):
                decisions = s.cycle(health={ES: payload, SS: payload,
                                            PS: payload})
                self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
                self.assertEqual([d.key for d in alerts(decisions)
                                  if d.key[1] in ("pid", "R3 warning")],
                                 [])


class TestWorkerRule(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_disabled_for_180_s_trips(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        late = {"0": worker(False, s.wall + 15 - 179), "1": worker(True)}
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(workers=late)), [])
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(workers={
            "0": worker(False, s.wall + 15 - 180), "1": worker(True)}))
        self.assertEqual((restart.program, restart.rule), (W0, "R4"))
        self.assertIn("disabled it for not answering 180 s ago",
                      restart.text)

    def test_a_worker_that_restarted_after_it_was_disabled_is_spared(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        s.set(W0, start_wall=s.wall + 15 - 60, pid=905)
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(workers={
            "0": worker(False, s.wall + 15 - 200), "1": worker(True)})),
            [])

    def test_a_worker_that_never_connects_trips(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(workers={"0": worker(False),
                                             "1": worker(True)}))
        self.assertEqual((restart.program, restart.rule), (W0, "R4"))

    def test_the_unconnected_rule_needs_es_up_and_another_worker(self):
        cases = (
            ({"0": worker(False), "1": worker(False)}, 9_999.0),
            ({"0": worker(False), "1": worker(True)}, 100.0),
        )
        for workers, uptime in cases:
            with self.subTest(workers=workers, uptime=uptime):
                s = Scenario()
                s.warm_up()
                health = {ES: {"pid": 104, "uptime_s": uptime,
                               "busy": []}}
                self.assertEqual(
                    actions(s.cycle(workers=workers, health=health)), [])

    def test_the_unconnected_rule_trips_at_300_s_of_running(self):
        for running_s, expected in ((299, []), (300, [(W0, "R4")])):
            with self.subTest(running_s=running_s):
                s = Scenario()
                s.warm_up()
                s.set(W0, start_wall=s.wall + 15 - running_s, pid=905)
                decisions = actions(s.cycle(
                    workers={"0": worker(False), "1": worker(True)}))
                self.assertEqual(
                    [(d.program, d.rule) for d in decisions], expected)

    def test_an_unhealthy_es_suppresses_the_rule(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        decisions = s.cycle(fail=[ES], workers={
            "0": worker(False, s.wall + 15 - 600), "1": worker(True)})
        self.assertEqual([d for d in actions(decisions)
                          if d.program == W0], [])

    def test_two_workers_tripping_together_give_one_alert(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        decisions = s.cycle(workers={
            "0": worker(False, s.wall + 15 - 600),
            "1": worker(False, s.wall + 15 - 600)})
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        [alert] = held(decisions, "workers")
        self.assertIn("2 Workers trip R4 together (cmsWorker 0, "
                      "cmsWorker 1)", alert.text)

    def test_the_workers_pool_is_separate(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for index, name in enumerate(("cmslogservice", "cmsscoringservice",
                                      "cmsproxyservice")):
            s.state.ledger.book(Booking(
                s.wall - 100 + index, name, name, SERVICES_POOL, RESTART,
                "R2", False, 1), False)
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(workers={"0": worker(False),
                                             "1": worker(True)}))
        self.assertEqual(restart.program, W0)


class TestWorkersEpisode(unittest.TestCase):
    """H4's one alert for the Workers that trip R4 together."""

    KEY = ("workers", "H4")

    @staticmethod
    def both(s: Scenario) -> dict:
        """Return ES's status of both Workers, disabled 600 s before."""
        disabled_at = s.wall + CYCLE_S - 600
        return {"0": worker(False, disabled_at),
                "1": worker(False, disabled_at)}

    @staticmethod
    def alone(s: Scenario) -> dict:
        """Return ES's status of Worker 0 disabled, Worker 1 fine."""
        return {"0": worker(False, s.wall + CYCLE_S - 600),
                "1": worker(True)}

    def grouped(self, decisions: list) -> bool:
        return any(d.key == self.KEY for d in decisions)

    def test_a_second_episode_is_entering_a_hold_again(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        [line] = [d for d in s.cycle(workers=self.both(s))
                  if d.key == self.KEY]
        self.assertEqual(line.holds, ("H4",))
        # The same episode: the rate limit applies.
        self.assertFalse(self.grouped(s.cycle(workers=self.both(s))))
        # The Workers recover: the episode ends after 2 quiet cycles.
        s.cycle()
        self.assertIn(self.KEY, s.state.held_headline)
        s.cycle()
        for table in (s.state.held_headline, s.state.alert_sent):
            self.assertNotIn(self.KEY, table)
        # A new episode, 60 s after the first line, is a state change.
        [line] = [d for d in s.cycle(workers=self.both(s))
                  if d.key == self.KEY]
        self.assertEqual(line.holds, ("H4",))

    def test_another_hold_heading_the_group_does_not_end_the_episode(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        lock = Gate(True, backends=lock_wait_snapshot())
        # Both Workers trip in every cycle; a lock wait comes and goes,
        # so H3 heads their trips every other cycle and H4 the others.
        history = [s.cycle(workers=self.both(s),
                           gate=lock if cycle % 2 else GREEN)
                   for cycle in range(int(ALERT_REPEAT_S / CYCLE_S) + 1)]
        self.assertEqual([line.holds[0] for line in held(history[1], W0)],
                         ["H3"])
        self.assertEqual([self.grouped(d) for d in history],
                         [True] + [False] * 19 + [True])

    def test_a_trip_that_comes_and_goes_does_not_end_the_episode(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        history = [s.cycle(workers=self.both(s) if cycle % 2 == 0 else None)
                   for cycle in range(int(ALERT_REPEAT_S / CYCLE_S) + 1)]
        self.assertEqual([self.grouped(d) for d in history],
                         [True] + [False] * 19 + [True])

    def test_a_worker_that_trips_alone_does_not_keep_the_episode(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        self.assertTrue(self.grouped(s.cycle(workers=self.both(s))))
        # Worker 1 recovers; Worker 0 trips alone: H4 does not apply to
        # it, so it is restarted, and these cycles are quiet ones.
        [restart] = actions(s.cycle(workers=self.alone(s)))
        self.assertEqual(restart.program, W0)
        self.assertIn(self.KEY, s.state.held_headline)
        s.cycle(workers=self.alone(s))
        self.assertNotIn(self.KEY, s.state.held_headline)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

```python
    def run(self) -> None:
        """Run the phases of the cycle, in order."""
        state = self.state
        if state.started_mono is None:
            state.started_mono = self.mono
        if state.summary_mono is None:
            state.summary_mono = self.mono
        state.ledger.prune(self.wall)
        self._track_gate()
        self._track_pause()
        self._track_programs()
        self._record_holder_cpu()
        if self.starved:
            state.near_miss.starved_cycles += 1
            return
        self._check_pids()
        self._liveness_trips()
        self._section_trips()
        self._worker_trips()
        self._act_on_trips()
        self._update_incidents()
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add these methods to `_Cycle`**, after `_liveness_trips`:

```python
    # R3.

    def _record_holder_cpu(self) -> None:
        """Keep the CPU samples of ES's holder thread (R3 (b))."""
        samples = [sample for sample in self.state.holder_samples
                   if self.mono - sample[0] <= ES_CPU_KEEP_S]
        if self.obs.holder_cpu is not None:
            tid, cpu_s = self.obs.holder_cpu
            samples.append((self.mono, tid, cpu_s))
        self.state.holder_samples = samples

    def _section_trips(self) -> None:
        """Apply R3 to the sections of ES, SS and PS (3.5).

        A section past half its trip adds its program to self.half (H4)
        and, if it does not trip, an early warning to self.warnings:
        _act_on_trips emits them with R1's and R2's.

        """
        for program in self.running:
            sections = busy_sections(self.obs.health.get(program.name))
            if not sections:
                continue
            if program.kind == "EvaluationService":
                self._es_section(program, sections[0])
            elif program.kind == "ScoringService":
                self._ss_section(program, sections)
            elif program.kind == "ProxyService":
                self._ps_section(program, sections)

    def _es_section(self, program: ProgramFacts, section: dict) -> None:
        """Apply R3 to ES's oldest section."""
        what, age_s = section["what"], section["age_s"]
        idle_s = section["idle_s"]
        if not what.startswith(ES_SECTION_PREFIX):
            return
        if what == INVALIDATION_SECTION:
            if age_s > SECTION_TRIP_S:
                self._alert(
                    (program.name, "re-evaluation"), "WARNING",
                    "Watchdog: re-evaluation still running for %d min in "
                    "%s; the watchdog never restarts it for that."
                    % (age_s // 60, program.label))
            return
        if age_s <= SECTION_WARN_S or idle_s < ES_IDLE_WARN_S:
            return
        self.half.add(program.name)
        if age_s > SECTION_TRIP_S and idle_s >= ES_IDLE_TRIP_S \
                and self._holder_idle(section["tid"]) \
                and not self._es_statement_running(program):
            self.trips.append(Trip(
                program, "R3", "its locked section %s has been held for "
                "%d s with no statement for %d s, under %d s of CPU in "
                "the last minute and no statement running in the database"
                % (what, age_s, idle_s, ES_CPU_MIN_S)))
            return
        self.warnings.append(Trip(
            program, "R3", "the locked section %s of %s has been held for "
            "%d s with no statement for %d s" % (
                what, program.label, age_s, idle_s)))

    def _holder_idle(self, tid: int | None) -> bool:
        """R3 (b): the holder used under 1 s of CPU in the last minute.

        It compares with the newest sample of the same thread that is
        at least ES_CPU_LOOKBACK_S old; without one, (b) is not met.

        """
        current = self.obs.holder_cpu
        if tid is None or current is None or current[0] != tid:
            return False
        old = [sample for sample in self.state.holder_samples
               if sample[1] == tid
               and self.mono - sample[0] >= ES_CPU_LOOKBACK_S]
        if not old:
            return False
        newest = max(old, key=lambda sample: sample[0])
        return current[1] - newest[2] < ES_CPU_MIN_S

    def _es_statement_running(self, program: ProgramFacts) -> bool:
        """R3 (c): whether a backend of ES is running a statement.

        A lock wait (left to H3) and a write to a client that does not
        read (ClientWrite) do not count; a NULL field counts as "not
        Lock" and "not ClientWrite". With a red gate there is no
        snapshot, and it counts as running.

        """
        if not self.obs.gate.green:
            return True
        return any(backend.application_name == program.label
                   and backend.state == "active"
                   and backend.wait_event_type != "Lock"
                   and backend.wait_event != "ClientWrite"
                   for backend in self.obs.gate.backends)

    def _ss_section(self, program: ProgramFacts,
                    sections: list[dict]) -> None:
        """Apply R3 to SS's scoring."""
        ages = [section["age_s"] for section in sections
                if section["what"] == "score"]
        if not ages or max(ages) <= SECTION_WARN_S:
            return
        self.half.add(program.name)
        if max(ages) > SECTION_TRIP_S:
            self.trips.append(Trip(program, "R3", "one scoring has run "
                                   "for %d s" % max(ages)))
            return
        self.warnings.append(Trip(
            program, "R3", "one scoring of %s has run for %d s"
            % (program.label, max(ages))))

    def _ps_section(self, program: ProgramFacts,
                    sections: list[dict]) -> None:
        """Apply R3 to PS's sends to the rankings."""
        idles = [section["idle_s"] for section in sections
                 if section["what"] == "send"]
        if not idles or max(idles) <= SECTION_WARN_S:
            return
        self.half.add(program.name)
        if max(idles) > SECTION_TRIP_S \
                and all(probe.ok for probe in self.obs.rankings.values()):
            self.trips.append(Trip(
                program, "R3", "a send to the rankings has started no "
                "request for %d s while every ranking answers"
                % max(idles)))
            return
        self.warnings.append(Trip(
            program, "R3", "a send of %s to the rankings has started no "
            "request for %d s" % (program.label, max(idles))))

    # R4.

    def _worker_trips(self) -> None:
        """Apply R4 to the Workers, from ES's workers_status() (3.5)."""
        es = next((program for program in self.running
                   if program.kind == "EvaluationService"), None)
        workers = self.obs.workers
        # An unhealthy ES suppresses R4: its view of the Workers is old.
        if es is None or not isinstance(workers, Mapping) \
                or not self.obs.rpc.get(es.name, Probe(False)).ok:
            return
        statuses = {str(shard): status for shard, status in workers.items()
                    if isinstance(status, Mapping)}
        es_health = self.obs.health.get(es.name)
        es_uptime = (es_health.get("uptime_s")
                     if isinstance(es_health, Mapping) else None)
        for program in self.running:
            if program.kind != "Worker":
                continue
            status = statuses.get(str(program.shard))
            if status is None:
                continue
            detail = self._disabled_too_long(program, status) \
                or self._never_connected(program, status, statuses,
                                         es_uptime)
            if detail:
                self.failing.add(program.name)
                self.trips.append(Trip(program, "R4", detail))

    def _disabled_too_long(self, program: ProgramFacts,
                           status: Mapping) -> str:
        """R4 (a): ES disabled it 180 s ago, and it did not restart."""
        disabled_at = status.get("disabled_at")
        if status.get("disabled_by_timeout") is not True \
                or not is_number(disabled_at) \
                or program.start_wall is None \
                or program.start_wall >= disabled_at:
            return ""
        disabled_for = age(self.wall, disabled_at)
        if disabled_for < WORKER_DISABLED_TRIP_S:
            return ""
        return ("EvaluationService disabled it for not answering %d s "
                "ago, and it has not restarted since" % disabled_for)

    def _never_connected(self, program: ProgramFacts, status: Mapping,
                         statuses: Mapping[str, Mapping],
                         es_uptime: object) -> str:
        """R4 (b): not connected after 300 s, while others are.

        A Worker that ES disabled less than WORKER_DISABLED_TRIP_S ago
        is left to (a): it has just been told to quit, and it
        disconnects as it does.

        """
        disabled_at = status.get("disabled_at")
        if status.get("disabled_by_timeout") is True \
                and is_number(disabled_at) \
                and age(self.wall, disabled_at) < WORKER_DISABLED_TRIP_S:
            return ""
        others = any(other.get("connected") is True
                     for shard, other in statuses.items()
                     if shard != str(program.shard))
        if status.get("connected") is not False or not others \
                or program.start_wall is None \
                or not is_number(es_uptime) \
                or es_uptime < ES_UP_FOR_WORKERS_S:
            return ""
        running_for = age(self.wall, program.start_wall)
        if running_for < WORKER_UNCONNECTED_TRIP_S:
            return ""
        return ("it has not connected to EvaluationService in %d s of "
                "running, while other Workers have" % running_for)
```

  - Replace `_act_on_trips`, `_early_warnings` and `_outlook` (from
    `def _act_on_trips` to the line before `def _holds`) with:

```python
    def _act_on_trips(self) -> None:
        """Turn each trip into an action, or into an alert if held."""
        self._early_warnings()
        held_workers: list[Trip] = []
        h4_on_workers = False
        incidents = self.state.ledger.incidents
        for trip in self.trips:
            program = trip.program
            opened = program.name not in incidents
            if opened:
                incidents[program.name] = {
                    "rule": trip.rule, "opened": self.wall, "passes": 0}
            shadow = self.state.shadow_for(program.klass)
            holds = self._holds(program, shadow)
            if program.kind == "Worker" \
                    and any(code == "H4" for code, _ in holds):
                h4_on_workers = True
            if holds and holds[0][0] == "H4" and program.kind == "Worker":
                held_workers.append(trip)
            elif holds:
                self._held(trip, holds, opened)
            else:
                self._restart(trip, shadow)
        if held_workers:
            self._held_workers(held_workers)
        self._track_workers_episode(h4_on_workers)

    def _track_workers_episode(self, h4_applied: bool) -> None:
        """End the Workers' H4 episode after PASSES_TO_RESOLVE quiet cycles.

        A quiet cycle is a non-starved one (this runs in those only) in
        which H4 applies to no Worker trip. A Worker that trips alone,
        or over its budget, does not keep the episode open; a trip that
        comes and goes, or another hold heading the group for a while,
        does not end it. The next episode is entering a hold again.

        h4_applied: whether H4 applied to a Worker trip in this cycle.

        """
        state = self.state
        if h4_applied or WORKERS_H4_KEY not in state.held_headline:
            state.workers_h4_quiet = 0
            return
        state.workers_h4_quiet += 1
        if state.workers_h4_quiet >= PASSES_TO_RESOLVE:
            state.workers_h4_quiet = 0
            for table in (state.held_headline, state.held_announced,
                          state.alert_sent):
                table.pop(WORKERS_H4_KEY, None)

    def _early_warnings(self) -> None:
        """Emit the early warnings of the cycle (3.5, 3.9)."""
        for warning in self.warnings:
            program = warning.program
            self._alert(
                (program.name, warning.rule + " warning"), "WARNING",
                "Watchdog: early warning: %s. %s"
                % (warning.detail,
                   self._outlook(program, self._trip_point(warning))))

    @staticmethod
    def _trip_point(warning: Trip) -> str:
        """Say when the rule of an early warning trips (3.5).

        warning: the early warning.

        return: the start of the sentence that says what happens then.

        """
        if warning.rule != "R3":
            return "At %d misses, the latest included" % MISSES_TO_TRIP
        kind = warning.program.kind
        if kind == "EvaluationService":
            return ("Past %d s, with no statement for %d s, no CPU used by "
                    "its thread and none of its statements running"
                    % (SECTION_TRIP_S, ES_IDLE_TRIP_S))
        if kind == "ProxyService":
            return ("Past %d s with no request, while every ranking "
                    "answers" % SECTION_TRIP_S)
        return "Past %d s" % SECTION_TRIP_S

    def _outlook(self, program: ProgramFacts, trip_point: str) -> str:
        """Say what happens if program goes on failing, and the next step.

        It does not promise a restart that observe mode, an H9 class or
        a hold that applies now would not give.

        program: the program with an early warning.
        trip_point: when its rule trips, as _trip_point says.

        return: the sentences to add to the warning.

        """
        shadow = self.state.shadow_for(program.klass)
        holds = self._holds(program, shadow)
        if self.state.mode == OBSERVE:
            what = "only logs the restart it would do (observe mode)"
        elif shadow:
            what = ("only logs the restart it would do (class %s is not "
                    "in CMS_WATCHDOG_RESTART)" % program.klass)
        elif holds:
            what = "would restart it"
        else:
            what = "restarts it"
        text = "%s, the watchdog %s." % (trip_point, what)
        if holds:
            text += " But %s applies now: %s." % holds[0]
            text += _other_holds(holds)
        return text + (" Check ./logs.sh for its log; cmsWatchdog "
                       "--snapshot %s writes its stacks." % program.name)
```

  `cms.db.POOL_TIMEOUT_S` (Task 5),
  `EvaluationService.SWEEPER_PERIOD_S` (Task 3),
  `cms.db.connect_options.LIBPQ_DEFAULTS`,
  `cms.service.ProxyService.CONNECT_TIMEOUT`, `READ_TIMEOUT`,
  `ProxyExecutor.MAX_RETRY_WAIT`, `cms.io.async_rpc.FIRE_AND_FORGET_TIMEOUT`.
- Produces:
  - the final `_Cycle.run()`;
  - START decisions: `Decision(START, programs=(...), rule="R5",
    would=..., shadow=..., key=("-", "R5"))`, at most one real batch
    and one H9 batch per cycle;
  - alert keys `("ranking <address>", "ranking")`, `(name, "absent")`
    (a program the `/proc` source lost: alert only), `(name, "help")`,
    `("-", "summary")` (INFO), `(name, "R5")`; a held R5 start goes
    through Task 9's `_held` as `Trip(program, "R5", "FATAL after a
    database outage")`, so its line reads `… fails R5 (FATAL after a
    database outage) but is held by H<n>: …` with `_other_holds`, opens
    the program's incident, and follows the hold-episode rate limits
    (the first H7 of an episode at once); a start, like a restart, ends
    the program's hold episodes (`_forget_episodes`);
  - `summary_text(stats: NearMiss) -> str`;
  - `program_status(state: PolicyState, observation: Observation,
    wall: float) -> list[dict]`, one entry per program:
    `{"name", "label", "kind", "state", "pid", "misses", "known",
    "http_ok", "http_latency_s", "rpc_ok", "rpc_latency_s", "sections",
    "incident", "restarts_last_hour", "shadow_restarts_last_hour"}`
    (Task 17 writes it into the status file; Task 21's harness reads
    it).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**
  - Create `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/policy_fatal_test.py`:

```python
# (fork header)

"""Tests for cmsWatchdog's policy: FATAL starts, rankings, the lines
that follow a restart, the hourly summary and the status."""

import unittest

from cms.watchdog.policy import CYCLE_S, INVALIDATION_SECTION, \
    OBSERVE, RESTART, START, Gate, NearMiss, Probe, program_status
from cmstestsuite.unit_tests.watchdog.scenario import AWS, CWS0, CWS1, \
    ES, LS, PS, RED, RS, SS, W0, W1, Scenario, actions, alerts, busy, \
    held, lock_wait_snapshot


def fatal(s: Scenario, *names: str) -> None:
    """Make names FATAL, as if they last started 5 s from now."""
    for name in names:
        s.set(name, state="FATAL", pid=None, start_wall=s.wall + 5)


def outage(s: Scenario, red_cycles: int = 2) -> None:
    """Run red cycles: a database outage."""
    for _ in range(red_cycles):
        s.cycle(gate=RED)


class TestFatalStarts(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_a_start_after_two_green_cycles(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        fatal(s, SS)
        outage(s)
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle()), [])
        [start] = actions(s.cycle())
        self.assertEqual(
            (start.action, start.programs, start.rule, start.would,
             start.shadow),
            (START, (SS,), "R5", False, False))
        self.assertIn(SS, s.state.ledger.incidents)

    def test_ten_fatal_programs_start_in_one_batch_infra_first(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        fatal(s, LS, RS, SS, ES, W0, W1, PS, CWS0, CWS1, AWS)
        outage(s)
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle()), [])
        [start] = actions(s.cycle())
        self.assertEqual(start.programs,
                         (LS, RS, ES, PS, SS, AWS, CWS0, CWS1, W0, W1))

    def test_a_program_fatal_with_the_database_up_is_left_to_a_human(
            self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        fatal(s, SS)
        decisions = s.cycle()
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        [alert] = [d for d in alerts(decisions) if d.key == (SS, "R5")]
        self.assertEqual(alert.level, "ERROR")
        self.assertIn("left to you", alert.text)

    def test_a_red_interval_before_its_last_start_does_not_count(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        outage(s)
        for _ in range(5):
            s.cycle()
        fatal(s, SS)
        for _ in range(3):
            self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle()), [])

    def test_it_ignores_h3_h4_h5_and_the_program_grace(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        fatal(s, SS)
        outage(s)
        noisy = {
            "gate": Gate(True, backends=lock_wait_snapshot()),
            "fail": [CWS0, CWS1, LS],
            "health": {ES: busy(104, "post_finish_lock:_x_sync", 100.0,
                                40.0),
                       PS: busy(107, "send", 500.0, 100.0)},
        }
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(**noisy)), [])
        [start] = actions(s.cycle(**noisy))
        self.assertEqual(start.programs, (SS,))

    def test_it_waits_for_the_watchdog_grace(self):
        s = Scenario()
        fatal(s, SS)
        s.cycle(gate=RED)
        s.cycle()
        history = [s.cycle() for _ in range(7)]
        self.assertEqual([bool(actions(d)) for d in history],
                         [False] * 6 + [True])
        [alert] = held(history[0], SS)
        self.assertIn("held by H6: the watchdog itself started 30 s ago",
                      alert.text)

    def test_a_held_start_is_a_held_trip(self):
        s = Scenario()
        fatal(s, SS)
        s.cycle(gate=RED)
        s.cycle()
        [alert] = held(s.cycle(supervisor_ok=False), SS)
        self.assertEqual(alert.holds, ("H1", "H6"))
        self.assertEqual(
            alert.text,
            "Watchdog: cmsScoringService 0 fails R5 (FATAL after a "
            "database outage) but is held by H1: supervisord did not "
            "answer in this cycle. Also: H6. Check ./status.sh.")
        self.assertEqual(s.state.ledger.incidents[SS]["rule"], "R5")

    def test_it_has_its_own_budget(self):
        s = Scenario(mode=OBSERVE)
        s.warm_up()
        fatal(s, SS)
        outage(s)
        s.cycle()
        self.assertTrue(actions(s.cycle())[0].would)
        self.assertTrue(actions(s.cycle())[0].would)
        decisions = s.cycle()
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        [alert] = held(decisions, SS)
        self.assertIn("held by H7: will not start cmsScoringService 0",
                      alert.text)
        self.assertEqual(s.state.ledger.count(SS, START, False, s.wall), 2)
        self.assertEqual(s.state.ledger.count(SS, RESTART, False, s.wall),
                         0)

    def test_h9_splits_the_batch(self):
        s = Scenario(restart=("web",))
        s.warm_up()
        fatal(s, LS, CWS0)
        outage(s)
        s.cycle()
        real, shadow = actions(s.cycle())
        self.assertEqual((real.programs, real.would, real.shadow),
                         ((CWS0,), False, False))
        self.assertEqual((shadow.programs, shadow.would, shadow.shadow),
                         ((LS,), True, True))

    def test_it_waits_for_an_action_in_flight(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        fatal(s, SS)
        outage(s)
        s.cycle()
        decisions = s.cycle(in_flight=True)
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        self.assertIn("held by H8", held(decisions, SS)[0].text)
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle())[0].programs, (SS,))


class TestRankings(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_a_ranking_that_does_not_answer_is_only_alerted(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        down = {"http://ranking:8890/": Probe(False, None,
                                              "Connection refused")}
        lines = []
        for _ in range(4):
            decisions = s.cycle(rankings=down)
            self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
            lines.append([d for d in alerts(decisions)
                          if d.key[1] == "ranking"])
        self.assertEqual([len(line) for line in lines], [0, 0, 1, 0])
        self.assertIn("ranking http://ranking:8890/ does not answer (3 "
                      "cycles in a row: Connection refused)",
                      lines[2][0].text)
        up = {"http://ranking:8890/": Probe(True, 0.02)}
        [resolved] = [d for d in s.cycle(rankings=up)
                      if d.text.startswith("Resolved:")]
        self.assertIn("ranking http://ranking:8890/ answers again",
                      resolved.text)


class TestAbsentPrograms(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_a_program_the_proc_source_lost_is_only_alerted(self):
        s = Scenario(mode=OBSERVE)
        s.warm_up()
        s.set(W0, state="ABSENT", pid=None)
        decisions = s.cycle()
        self.assertEqual(actions(decisions), [])
        [alert] = [d for d in alerts(decisions) if d.key == (W0, "absent")]
        self.assertIn("cmsWorker 0 is gone", alert.text)


class TestDidItHelp(unittest.TestCase):

    def restarted(self) -> Scenario:
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        self.assertEqual(actions(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]))[0].program, CWS1)
        return s

    @staticmethod
    def help_lines(decisions: list) -> list[str]:
        return [d.text for d in decisions if d.key[1] == "help"]

    def test_the_first_pass_after_the_restart(self):
        s = self.restarted()
        s.set(CWS1, pid=999, start_wall=s.wall + 5)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.help_lines(s.cycle()),
            ["Watchdog: cmsContestWebServer 1 answers again 15 s after "
             "the restart."])
        self.assertEqual(self.help_lines(s.cycle()), [])

    def test_still_failing_120_s_after_the_restart(self):
        s = self.restarted()
        history = [self.help_lines(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1]))
                   for _ in range(8)]
        self.assertEqual(history[:7], [[]] * 7)
        self.assertEqual(len(history[7]), 1)
        self.assertIn("cmsContestWebServer 1 is still failing 120 s after "
                      "the restart", history[7][0])

    def test_no_line_follows_a_would_action(self):
        s = Scenario(mode=OBSERVE)
        s.warm_up()
        for _ in range(4):
            s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        for _ in range(10):
            self.assertEqual(self.help_lines(s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])), [])


class TestSummaryAndStatus(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_the_hourly_summary(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        s.cycle(fail=[CWS1])
        s.cycle(health={
            ES: busy(104, "post_finish_lock:_write_results_sync", 100.0,
                     40.0),
            SS: busy(103, "score", 50.0, 50.0),
            PS: busy(107, "send", 60.0, 20.0)})
        s.cycle(lag_s=6.0)
        s.cycle(gate=RED)
        s.cycle()
        decisions = s.cycle(advance=3600.0, since=CYCLE_S)
        [summary] = [d for d in decisions if d.key == ("-", "summary")]
        self.assertEqual(summary.level, "INFO")
        for part in (
                "cmsContestWebServer 1 at most 1 misses in 6, p99 0.01 s",
                "longest ES section 100 s "
                "(post_finish_lock:_write_results_sync)",
                "longest ES idle_s in a section 40 s",
                "ES sections past half their trip: "
                "post_finish_lock:_write_results_sync held 100 s from "
                "08:00 UTC, up to 40 s with no statement",
                "longest SS execution 50 s;",
                "longest PS idle_s in a send 20 s",
                "1 red-gate cycles, 1 starved cycles"):
            self.assertIn(part, summary.text)
        self.assertEqual(s.state.near_miss, NearMiss())

    def test_the_summary_lists_every_es_section_once(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        for index in range(3):
            # The same section, 15 s older each cycle.
            s.cycle(health={ES: busy(
                104, "post_finish_lock:_write_results_sync",
                100.0 + 15 * index, 40.0 + 15 * index)})
        for _ in range(24):
            # A new section each cycle: the same age, a later start.
            s.cycle(health={ES: busy(104, "post_finish_lock:_x_sync",
                                     95.0, 35.0)})
        [summary] = [d for d in s.cycle(advance=3600.0, since=CYCLE_S)
                     if d.key == ("-", "summary")]
        self.assertEqual(summary.text.count("_write_results_sync held"), 1)
        self.assertIn("_write_results_sync held 130 s from 08:00 UTC, up "
                      "to 70 s with no statement", summary.text)
        self.assertEqual(summary.text.count("_x_sync held 95 s"), 24)

    def test_the_summary_marks_a_scoring_during_a_re_evaluation(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        s.cycle(health={
            ES: busy(104, INVALIDATION_SECTION, 400.0, 400.0),
            SS: busy(103, "score", 70.0, 70.0)})
        [summary] = [d for d in s.cycle(advance=3600.0, since=CYCLE_S)
                     if d.key == ("-", "summary")]
        self.assertIn("longest ES section 400 s "
                      "(post_finish_lock:_invalidate_submission_sync, a "
                      "re-evaluation)", summary.text)
        self.assertIn("longest SS execution 70 s, overlapping a "
                      "re-evaluation", summary.text)
        self.assertIn("longest ES idle_s in a section 0 s", summary.text)

    def test_program_status(self):
        s = Scenario()
        s.warm_up()
        s.cycle(fail=[CWS1], health={SS: busy(103, "score", 50.0, 50.0)})
        entries = {entry["name"]: entry for entry in
                   program_status(s.state, s.observation, s.wall)}
        cws1 = entries[CWS1]
        self.assertEqual(
            (cws1["label"], cws1["state"], cws1["misses"], cws1["known"],
             cws1["http_ok"], cws1["rpc_ok"], cws1["http_latency_s"]),
            ("cmsContestWebServer 1", "RUNNING", 1, 6, False, False,
             None))
        self.assertEqual(entries[CWS0]["http_latency_s"], 0.01)
        self.assertEqual([section["what"]
                          for section in entries[SS]["sections"]],
                         ["score"])
        self.assertEqual(entries[W0]["misses"], 0)
        self.assertIsNone(entries[W0]["http_ok"])
        self.assertEqual(entries[CWS1]["restarts_last_hour"], 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

  - Create `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/policy_invariants_test.py`:

```python
# (fork header)

"""The invariants of cmsWatchdog's policy, over every scenario (#38).

- At most one action per cycle: one restart, or the R5 batch (with its
  H9 part); never both.
- No action in a cycle whose gate is red, or while another is in
  flight.
- Observe mode and enforce mode with every class enabled decide the
  same, apart from the actuator: the would flag, the "did it help"
  lines, which follow real restarts only, and the early warnings, which
  say what the mode does at the trip (they are compared by key).

No script has a lock wait whose blocker is a session of a program the
watchdog restarted: H3's "orphan of pid N" mark follows real restarts
only (Ledger.last_restart_of leaves would-actions out), so the two
modes word that line differently by design.

"""

import unittest
from dataclasses import replace

from cms.watchdog.policy import ENFORCE, OBSERVE, RESTART, START
from cmstestsuite.unit_tests.watchdog.scenario import CWS0, CWS1, ES, \
    RED, SS, WALL0, Scenario, worker


ES_STUCK = {"pid": 104, "uptime_s": 9_999.0, "busy": [{
    "what": "post_finish_lock:_write_results_sync", "tid": 4321,
    "age_s": 400.0, "statements": 3, "idle_s": 300.0, "stack": None}]}


def fatal_ss(s: Scenario) -> None:
    s.set(SS, state="FATAL", pid=None, start_wall=s.wall + 20)


# Each scenario: a setup (or None) and the keyword arguments of each
# cycle after Scenario.warm_up().
SCRIPTS = {
    "r1": (None, [{"fail": [CWS1]}] * 45),
    "livelock": (None, ([{"fail": [CWS1]}] * 2 + [{}]
                        + [{"fail": [CWS1]}] * 3) * 6),
    "correlated": (None, [{"fail": [CWS0, CWS1]}] * 20),
    "outage": (None, [{"fail": [CWS1]}] * 6
               + [{"fail": [CWS1], "gate": RED}] * 5
               + [{"fail": [CWS1]}] * 25),
    "es stuck": (None, [{"health": {ES: ES_STUCK},
                         "holder_cpu": (4321, 5.0)}] * 30),
    "worker": (None, [{"workers": {"0": worker(False, WALL0),
                                   "1": worker(True)}}] * 30),
    "in flight": (None, [{"fail": [CWS1], "in_flight": True},
                         {"fail": [CWS1]}] * 15),
    "fatal": (fatal_ss, [{"gate": RED}] * 3 + [{}] * 12),
    "fatal and r1": (fatal_ss, [{"gate": RED, "fail": [CWS1]}] * 3
                     + [{"fail": [CWS1]}] * 20),
}


def play(mode: str, name: str) -> list[tuple[dict, list]]:
    """Play a script; return each cycle's arguments and decisions."""
    setup, cycles = SCRIPTS[name]
    s = Scenario(mode=mode)
    s.warm_up()
    if setup is not None:
        setup(s)
    return [(kwargs, s.cycle(**kwargs)) for kwargs in cycles]


def comparable(decisions: list) -> list:
    """Drop what only the mode explains (see the module docstring)."""
    return [replace(d, would=False,
                    text="" if d.key[1].endswith(" warning") else d.text)
            for d in decisions if d.key[1] != "help"]


class TestInvariants(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_at_most_one_action_per_cycle(self):
        for name in SCRIPTS:
            for mode in (OBSERVE, ENFORCE):
                for kwargs, decisions in play(mode, name):
                    restarts = [d for d in decisions if d.action == RESTART]
                    starts = [d for d in decisions if d.action == START]
                    with self.subTest(script=name, mode=mode):
                        self.assertLessEqual(len(restarts), 1)
                        self.assertFalse(restarts and starts)
                        self.assertLessEqual(
                            len([d for d in starts if not d.shadow]), 1)
                        self.assertTrue(all(d.rule == "R5" for d in starts))

    def test_no_action_while_the_gate_is_red_or_one_is_in_flight(self):
        for name in SCRIPTS:
            for mode in (OBSERVE, ENFORCE):
                for kwargs, decisions in play(mode, name):
                    blocked = kwargs.get("gate") is RED \
                        or kwargs.get("in_flight", False)
                    acted = [d for d in decisions if d.action != "alert"]
                    with self.subTest(script=name, mode=mode):
                        self.assertFalse(blocked and acted)

    def test_observe_and_enforce_decide_the_same(self):
        for name in SCRIPTS:
            observe = play(OBSERVE, name)
            enforce = play(ENFORCE, name)
            for index, ((_, seen), (_, done)) in enumerate(
                    zip(observe, enforce)):
                with self.subTest(script=name, cycle=index):
                    self.assertEqual(comparable(seen), comparable(done))

    def test_a_real_restart_gives_the_grace_of_a_would_restart(self):
        # H6's grace after the last action follows real actions too: the
        # cycle after the restart is held the same way in both modes.
        for mode in (OBSERVE, ENFORCE):
            with self.subTest(mode=mode):
                history = [decisions for _, decisions in play(mode, "r1")]
                first = next(index for index, decisions
                             in enumerate(history)
                             if any(d.action == RESTART for d in decisions))
                self.assertEqual([d.would for d in history[first]
                                  if d.action == RESTART],
                                 [mode == OBSERVE])
                [line] = [d for d in history[first + 1] if d.holds]
                self.assertEqual(line.holds[0], "H6")
                self.assertIn("its last action or would-action was 15 s "
                              "ago", line.text)

    def test_every_script_acts_at_least_once(self):
        # Otherwise the invariants above would hold trivially.
        for name in SCRIPTS:
            if name == "correlated":
                continue
            with self.subTest(script=name):
                self.assertTrue(any(
                    d.action != "alert"
                    for _, decisions in play(ENFORCE, name)
                    for d in decisions))
```

  - Create `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/ladder_test.py`:

```python
# (fork header)

"""Pin cmsWatchdog's thresholds against the timeouts of CMS (#38).

Each trip must come after the timeout that heals the same failure by
itself: a watchdog that restarts first would restart programs about to
recover. Moving a rung of this ladder fails a test that names
cms/watchdog/policy.py.

"""

import unittest

from cms.db import POOL_TIMEOUT_S
from cms.db.connect_options import LIBPQ_DEFAULTS
from cms.io.async_rpc import FIRE_AND_FORGET_TIMEOUT
from cms.service.EvaluationService import EvaluationService
from cms.service.ProxyService import CONNECT_TIMEOUT, READ_TIMEOUT, \
    ProxyExecutor
from cms.watchdog.policy import CYCLE_S, HTTP_TIMEOUT_S, MISSES_TO_TRIP, \
    RPC_TIMEOUT_S, SECTION_TRIP_S, WORKER_DISABLED_TRIP_S


WATCHDOG = "cmsWatchdog (cms/watchdog/policy.py)"


class TestLadder(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_the_earliest_liveness_trip_beats_the_39_ladder(self):
        # A pooled session waits POOL_TIMEOUT_S for a connection, and a
        # dead connection fails after tcp_user_timeout (milliseconds).
        ladder = max(POOL_TIMEOUT_S,
                     int(LIBPQ_DEFAULTS["tcp_user_timeout"]) / 1000)
        for name, timeout in (("R1", HTTP_TIMEOUT_S),
                              ("R2", RPC_TIMEOUT_S)):
            earliest = (MISSES_TO_TRIP - 1) * CYCLE_S + timeout
            self.assertGreater(
                earliest, ladder,
                "%s: %s trips after %s s, before the %s s timeouts of "
                "#39" % (WATCHDOG, name, earliest, ladder))

    def test_the_es_trip_exceeds_the_sweeper(self):
        self.assertGreater(
            SECTION_TRIP_S, EvaluationService.SWEEPER_PERIOD_S,
            "%s: R3 must outlast ES's sweeper period" % WATCHDOG)

    def test_the_ps_trip_exceeds_one_request_and_the_retry_wait(self):
        self.assertGreater(
            SECTION_TRIP_S, CONNECT_TIMEOUT + READ_TIMEOUT,
            "%s: R3 for PS must outlast one request" % WATCHDOG)
        self.assertGreater(
            SECTION_TRIP_S, ProxyExecutor.MAX_RETRY_WAIT,
            "%s: R3 for PS must outlast PS's retry wait" % WATCHDOG)

    def test_the_worker_rule_waits_for_es_quit(self):
        self.assertGreater(
            WORKER_DISABLED_TRIP_S, FIRE_AND_FORGET_TIMEOUT,
            "%s: R4 must wait for ES's own quit" % WATCHDOG)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Replace `_Cycle.run`** with its final form:

```python
    def run(self) -> None:
        """Run the phases of the cycle, in order."""
        state = self.state
        if state.started_mono is None:
            state.started_mono = self.mono
        if state.summary_mono is None:
            state.summary_mono = self.mono
        state.ledger.prune(self.wall)
        self._track_gate()
        self._track_pause()
        self._track_programs()
        self._record_holder_cpu()
        self._near_miss()
        if self.starved:
            state.near_miss.starved_cycles += 1
            self._summary()
            return
        self._check_pids()
        self._liveness_trips()
        self._section_trips()
        self._worker_trips()
        self._ranking_alerts()
        self._absent_alerts()
        # Before the actions: a restart decided in this cycle would
        # replace the one whose outcome this line reports.
        self._did_it_help()
        # Before the restarts: after an outage, starting what it left
        # FATAL comes first, and a restart in the same cycle gets H8.
        self._fatal_starts()
        self._act_on_trips()
        self._update_incidents()
        self._summary()
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add these methods to `_Cycle`**, after
  `_never_connected`:

```python
    # Rankings.

    def _ranking_alerts(self) -> None:
        """Alert about a ranking that does not answer (alert only)."""
        misses = self.state.ranking_misses
        for address, probe in self.obs.rankings.items():
            previous = misses.get(address, 0)
            misses[address] = 0 if probe.ok else previous + 1
            key = ("ranking " + address, "ranking")
            if misses[address] >= RANKING_MISSES_TO_ALERT:
                self._alert(
                    key, "WARNING",
                    "Watchdog: ranking %s does not answer (%d cycles in a "
                    "row: %s). The watchdog cannot restart it: check the "
                    "ranking container with ./status.sh and ./logs.sh "
                    "ranking." % (address, misses[address],
                                  probe.error or "no detail"),
                    change=misses[address] == RANKING_MISSES_TO_ALERT,
                    keep_open=True)
            elif probe.ok and previous >= RANKING_MISSES_TO_ALERT:
                self._resolve_key(
                    key, "Resolved: ranking %s answers again." % address)

    def _absent_alerts(self) -> None:
        """Alert about a program the /proc source no longer sees (3.2).

        Only the harness's /proc source reports ABSENT; it cannot start
        anything, so this is an alert and nothing else.

        """
        for program in self.obs.programs:
            if program.managed and program.state == "ABSENT":
                self._alert(
                    (program.name, "absent"), "WARNING",
                    "Watchdog: %s is gone: no process runs it any more, "
                    "and the /proc source cannot start it." % program.label)

    # R5.

    def _fatal_starts(self) -> None:
        """Apply R5: start what a database outage left FATAL (3.5).

        R5 obeys H1, H2 (its own FATAL_GREEN_CYCLES of green, read from
        green_cycles), H6 (the watchdog's own startup only), H7 (the
        FATAL budget), H8 and H9. A held start is a held trip: it opens
        the program's incident, and _held writes its line under the
        same rules as R1-R4's ("... fails R5 (FATAL after a database
        outage) but is held by H<n>: ...").

        """
        eligible = []
        for program in self.obs.programs:
            if not program.managed or program.state != "FATAL":
                continue
            if program.start_wall is not None \
                    and self.state.ledger.red_since(
                        program.start_wall - FATAL_RED_LOOKBACK_S):
                eligible.append(program)
            else:
                self._alert(
                    (program.name, "R5"), "ERROR",
                    "Watchdog: %s is FATAL and the database answered when "
                    "it last started: it is left to you. Read ./logs.sh, "
                    "fix the cause, then start it with supervisorctl."
                    % program.label)
        if not eligible or not self.obs.gate.green \
                or self.state.green_cycles < FATAL_GREEN_CYCLES:
            return
        since_start = self.mono - self.state.started_mono
        incidents = self.state.ledger.incidents
        batches: dict[bool, list[ProgramFacts]] = {False: [], True: []}
        for program in sorted(eligible, key=lambda p: (
                CLASS_ORDER.index(p.klass), p.name)):
            shadow = self.state.shadow_for(program.klass)
            holds = []
            if not self.obs.supervisor_ok:
                holds.append(("H1",
                              "supervisord did not answer in this cycle"))
            if since_start < GRACE_S:
                holds.append(("H6", "the watchdog itself started %d s ago"
                              % since_start))
            budget = self._budget(program, START, shadow)
            if budget:
                holds.append(("H7", budget))
            if self.acted or self.obs.in_flight:
                holds.append(("H8", "another action is in flight"))
            opened = program.name not in incidents
            if opened:
                incidents[program.name] = {
                    "rule": "R5", "opened": self.wall, "passes": 0}
            if holds:
                self._held(Trip(program, "R5",
                                "FATAL after a database outage"),
                           holds, opened)
            else:
                batches[shadow].append(program)
        for shadow in (False, True):
            batch = batches[shadow]
            if not batch:
                continue
            would = self.state.mode == OBSERVE or shadow
            for program in batch:
                self.state.ledger.book(Booking(
                    self.wall, program.name, program.label,
                    pool_of(program.klass), START, "R5", would), shadow)
                # Like a restart, a start ends the program's hold
                # episodes.
                self._forget_episodes(program.name)
            self.acted = True
            self.decisions.append(Decision(
                START, text="FATAL after a database outage",
                key=("-", "R5"),
                programs=tuple(program.name for program in batch),
                rule="R5", would=would, shadow=shadow))

    # After a restart.

    def _did_it_help(self) -> None:
        """Say whether each restart helped (3.7, step 8).

        At the first pass of a new pid, "X answers again"; after
        DID_IT_HELP_S without one, "X is still failing". Would-actions
        get no line: nothing was restarted.

        """
        tripped = {trip.program.name for trip in self.trips}
        by_name = {program.name: program for program in self.obs.programs}
        for name, program_state in self.state.programs.items():
            if program_state.restarted_mono is None:
                continue
            program = by_name.get(name)
            label = program.label if program is not None else name
            elapsed = self.mono - program_state.restarted_mono
            if program is not None and program.state == "RUNNING" \
                    and program.pid != program_state.restarted_pid \
                    and name not in self.failing and name not in tripped:
                text = "Watchdog: %s answers again %d s after the " \
                    "restart." % (label, elapsed)
            elif elapsed >= DID_IT_HELP_S:
                text = ("Watchdog: %s is still failing %d s after the "
                        "restart: look at its evidence file and "
                        "./logs.sh." % (label, elapsed))
            else:
                continue
            program_state.restarted_mono = None
            self.decisions.append(Decision(ALERT, text=text,
                                           key=(name, "help")))

    # The hourly summary.

    def _near_miss(self) -> None:
        """Feed this hour's near-miss statistics (starved cycles too)."""
        stats = self.state.near_miss
        invalidating = any(
            sections and sections[0]["what"] == INVALIDATION_SECTION
            for sections in (busy_sections(self.obs.health.get(program.name))
                             for program in self.running
                             if program.kind == "EvaluationService"))
        for program in self.running:
            window = self.state.programs[program.name].window
            probe = self._liveness_probe(program)
            if probe is not None:
                stats.misses[program.label] = max(
                    stats.misses.get(program.label, 0), sum(window))
                if probe.ok and probe.latency_s is not None:
                    stats.latencies.setdefault(program.label, []).append(
                        probe.latency_s)
            sections = busy_sections(self.obs.health.get(program.name))
            if program.kind == "EvaluationService" and sections:
                self._es_near_miss(sections[0])
            for section in sections:
                if program.kind == "ScoringService" \
                        and section["what"] == "score":
                    key = "%s@%d" % (section["tid"], round(
                        (self.wall - section["age_s"]) / 10))
                    if invalidating:
                        stats.ss_overlapping.add(key)
                    if stats.ss_longest is None \
                            or section["age_s"] > stats.ss_longest[0]:
                        stats.ss_longest = (section["age_s"], key)
                if program.kind == "ProxyService" \
                        and section["what"] == "send":
                    stats.ps_idle_s = max(stats.ps_idle_s,
                                          section["idle_s"])

    def _es_near_miss(self, section: dict) -> None:
        """Feed the statistics of ES's oldest section."""
        stats = self.state.near_miss
        what = section["what"]
        if not what.startswith(ES_SECTION_PREFIX):
            return
        if stats.es_longest is None or section["age_s"] > \
                stats.es_longest[0]:
            stats.es_longest = (section["age_s"], what)
        if what == INVALIDATION_SECTION:
            return
        stats.es_idle_s = max(stats.es_idle_s, section["idle_s"])
        if section["age_s"] > SECTION_WARN_S \
                and section["idle_s"] >= ES_IDLE_WARN_S:
            # One entry per section, with its largest values: the key is
            # its holder and its start (as for the SS executions).
            start = self.wall - section["age_s"]
            key = "%s@%d" % (section["tid"], round(start / 10))
            _, start, age_s, idle_s = stats.es_half.get(
                key, (what, start, 0.0, 0.0))
            stats.es_half[key] = (what, start,
                                  max(age_s, section["age_s"]),
                                  max(idle_s, section["idle_s"]))

    def _summary(self) -> None:
        """Emit the hourly near-miss summary at INFO, and start over."""
        if self.mono - self.state.summary_mono < SUMMARY_PERIOD_S:
            return
        self.decisions.append(Decision(
            ALERT, level="INFO", key=("-", "summary"),
            text=summary_text(self.state.near_miss)))
        self.state.near_miss = NearMiss()
        self.state.summary_mono = self.mono
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the two module functions** at the end of
  `cms/watchdog/policy.py`:

```python


def summary_text(stats: NearMiss) -> str:
    """Return the line of the hourly near-miss summary (3.9).

    stats: this hour's statistics.

    return: the line.

    """
    parts = []
    for label in sorted(stats.misses):
        latency = p99(stats.latencies.get(label, []))
        parts.append("%s at most %d misses in 6, p99 %s" % (
            label, stats.misses[label],
            "n/a" if latency is None else "%.2f s" % latency))
    if stats.es_longest is not None:
        age_s, what = stats.es_longest
        parts.append("longest ES section %d s (%s%s)" % (
            age_s, what,
            ", a re-evaluation" if what == INVALIDATION_SECTION else ""))
    parts.append("longest ES idle_s in a section %d s" % stats.es_idle_s)
    if stats.es_half:
        parts.append("ES sections past half their trip: %s" % "; ".join(
            "%s held %d s from %s, up to %d s with no statement" % (
                what, age_s, hhmm_utc(start), idle_s)
            for what, start, age_s, idle_s in sorted(
                stats.es_half.values(), key=lambda entry: entry[1])))
    if stats.ss_longest is not None:
        age_s, key = stats.ss_longest
        parts.append("longest SS execution %d s%s" % (
            age_s, ", overlapping a re-evaluation"
            if key in stats.ss_overlapping else ""))
    parts.append("longest PS idle_s in a send %d s" % stats.ps_idle_s)
    parts.append("%d red-gate cycles, %d starved cycles"
                 % (stats.red_cycles, stats.starved_cycles))
    return "Watchdog: hourly near-miss summary: %s." % "; ".join(parts)


def program_status(state: PolicyState, observation: Observation,
                   wall: float) -> list[dict]:
    """Describe each program for the status file (3.10).

    state: the state decide() returned for this cycle.
    observation: this cycle's observation.
    wall: now (epoch seconds).

    return: one dict per program of the process source.

    """
    entries = []
    for program in observation.programs:
        program_state = state.programs.get(program.name)
        http = observation.http.get(program.name)
        rpc = observation.rpc.get(program.name)
        entries.append({
            "name": program.name,
            "label": program.label if program.managed else None,
            "kind": program.kind,
            "state": program.state,
            "pid": program.pid,
            "misses": sum(program_state.window) if program_state else 0,
            "known": len(program_state.window) if program_state else 0,
            "http_ok": None if http is None else http.ok,
            "http_latency_s": None if http is None else http.latency_s,
            "rpc_ok": None if rpc is None else rpc.ok,
            "rpc_latency_s": None if rpc is None else rpc.latency_s,
            "sections": busy_sections(observation.health.get(program.name)),
            "incident": state.ledger.incidents.get(program.name),
            "restarts_last_hour": state.ledger.count(
                program.name, RESTART, False, wall),
            "shadow_restarts_last_hour": state.ledger.count(
                program.name, RESTART, True, wall),
        })
    return entries
```

### Task 12: The HTTP, ranking and RPC probes

**Files:**
- Create: `cms/watchdog/probes.py`
- Create: `cmstestsuite/unit_tests/watchdog/probes_network_test.py`
  (asyncio group)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from policy (probes take their timeouts as
  arguments; `main.py` passes the policy constants).
- Produces (in `cms/watchdog/probes.py`):
  - `ProbeResult(ok: bool, latency_s: float | None = None,
    error: str = "", data: Any = None)` (frozen);
  - `probe_host(address: str) -> str` (`""` and `"0.0.0.0"` become
    `"127.0.0.1"`); `describe(error: BaseException) -> str`;
  - `async http_probe(host, port, path, timeout, *, any_status=False,
    tls=False) -> ProbeResult`;
  - `RankingTarget(host: str, port: int, tls: bool)` with the property
    `visible` (`"http://host:port/"`, no credentials);
    `ranking_target(url: str) -> RankingTarget | None`;
  - `class RpcProbe(host: str, port: int, timeout: float)` with
    `async call(method: str, data: dict | None = None) -> ProbeResult`
    (the answer in `data`), `close()`, `connected: bool`;
    `RemoteError(ValueError)`; `MAX_MESSAGE_SIZE`.
