    'watchdogstats'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `cmstestsuite/loadtest/watchdogstats.py`:**

```python
# (fork header)

"""Check the pass criteria of a watchdog run (run.sh --watchdog, #38).

Usage: watchdogstats.py out/<run> --cws N --workers N

It reads watchdog_status.jsonl (one status per cycle, written by
cmsWatchdog --status-history), watchdog.log (its stdout), the driver's
requests.jsonl and, after a fault run, faults.json. It prints one
markdown row per criterion of "Harness L" in
docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-watchdog-design.md, then the probe
latencies, and exits 1 if a criterion fails. A fault run (one with
faults.json) is judged by the criteria of L3: its latency and near-miss
rows, the criteria of L1 and L2, are only recorded. Stdlib only: the
constants below copy those of cms/watchdog/policy.py, and a test keeps
them equal.

"""

import argparse
import calendar
import json
import math
import os
import re
import sys
import time


HTTP_TIMEOUT_S = 5.0
RPC_TIMEOUT_S = 3.0
# Half of each trip: the near-miss gates.
HALF_MISSES = 2
HALF_SECTION_S = 90.0
HALF_ES_IDLE_S = 30.0
INVALIDATION = "post_finish_lock:_invalidate_submission_sync"
# A fault shows in the gate from up to one gate probe (7 s) before it
# to one cycle and one probe (22 s) after it.
FAULT_BEFORE_S = 7.0
FAULT_AFTER_S = 22.0
WOULD = re.compile(r"\bWOULD (restart|start)\b")
HELD = re.compile(r"\bheld by H\d\b")
LINE_TIME = re.compile(r"^(\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d \d\d:\d\d:\d\d) UTC ")

Row = tuple[str, str, str, bool]


def load_jsonl(path: str) -> list[dict]:
    """Read a JSON-lines file; [] if it is missing."""
    try:
        with open(path) as f:
            return [json.loads(line) for line in f if line.strip()]
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return []


def p_quantile(values: list[float], q: float) -> float | None:
    """Return the q quantile of values (nearest rank), or None."""
    if not values:
        return None
    ordered = sorted(values)
    return ordered[max(0, math.ceil(q * len(ordered)) - 1)]


def line_time(line: str) -> float | None:
    """Return the epoch time of a watchdog.log line, or None."""
    match = LINE_TIME.match(line)
    if match is None:
        return None
    return float(calendar.timegm(time.strptime(match.group(1),
                                               "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")))


def fault_windows(run_dir: str) -> list[tuple[float, float]]:
    """Return the windows of F2 and F3, widened by the gate's slack."""
    try:
        with open(os.path.join(run_dir, "faults.json")) as f:
            records = json.load(f)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return []
    return [(record["start"] - FAULT_BEFORE_S,
             record["end"] + FAULT_AFTER_S)
            for record in records
            if record.get("fault") in ("F2", "F3") and "end" in record]


def inside(moment: float, windows: list[tuple[float, float]]) -> bool:
    return any(start <= moment <= end for start, end in windows)


def expected_labels(cws: int, workers: int) -> list[str]:
    """Return the labels of the programs every cycle must show."""
    return (["cmsLogService 0", "cmsEvaluationService 0",
             "cmsScoringService 0", "cmsProxyService 0",
             "cmsAdminWebServer 0"]
            + ["cmsContestWebServer %d" % i for i in range(cws)]
            + ["cmsWorker %d" % i for i in range(workers)])


def check(run_dir: str, cws: int, workers: int) -> tuple[list[Row],
                                                         list[str]]:
    """Return the rows of the criteria, and the latency lines."""
    statuses = load_jsonl(os.path.join(run_dir, "watchdog_status.jsonl"))
    try:
        with open(os.path.join(run_dir, "watchdog.log")) as f:
            lines = f.read().splitlines()
    except FileNotFoundError:
        lines = []
    requests = load_jsonl(os.path.join(run_dir, "requests.jsonl"))
    start = min((request["t"] for request in requests), default=None)
    windows = fault_windows(run_dir)
    fault_run = os.path.exists(os.path.join(run_dir, "faults.json"))
    rows: list[Row] = []

    woulds = [line for line in lines if WOULD.search(line)]
    rows.append(("no WOULD line", str(len(woulds)), "0", not woulds))

    held = [line for line in lines if HELD.search(line)]
    outside = [line for line in held
               if not inside(line_time(line) or 0.0, windows)]
    rows.append(("holds only inside F2 and F3", "%d of %d"
                 % (len(outside), len(held)), "0 outside", not outside))

    red = [item["time"] for item in statuses if item.get("gate") != "green"]
    red_outside = [moment for moment in red if not inside(moment, windows)]
    rows.append(("gate red only inside F2 and F3", "%d of %d cycles"
                 % (len(red_outside), len(red)), "0 outside",
                 not red_outside))

    problems = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    cycles = [item for item in statuses
              if start is not None and item["time"] >= start]
    for item in cycles:
        running = {program["label"] for program in item["programs"]
                   if program.get("state") == "RUNNING"}
        for label in expected_labels(cws, workers):
            if label in running:
                seen.add(label)
            elif label in seen or label != "cmsProxyService 0":
                problems.append("%s missing at %d" % (label, item["time"]))
    never = [label for label in expected_labels(cws, workers)
             if label not in seen]
    rows.append(("every program in every cycle",
                 "; ".join((["never: " + ", ".join(never)] if never else [])
                           + problems[:3]) or "all",
                 "all, from the driver's start",
                 bool(cycles) and not never and not problems))

    http: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
    rpc: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
    probed: set[str] = set()
    max_misses = 0
    es_bad: list[str] = []
    es_idle = 0.0
    ss_bad: list[str] = []
    ps_bad: list[str] = []
    for item in statuses:
        invalidating = any(
            section.get("what") == INVALIDATION
            for program in item["programs"]
            if program.get("kind") == "EvaluationService"
            for section in program.get("sections") or [])
        for program in item["programs"]:
            label = program.get("label") or program["name"]
            for key, table in (("http", http), ("rpc", rpc)):
                if program.get(key + "_ok") is not None:
                    probed.add(label)
                if program.get(key + "_latency_s") is not None:
                    table.setdefault(label, []).append(
                        program[key + "_latency_s"])
            max_misses = max(max_misses, program.get("misses") or 0)
            for section in program.get("sections") or []:
                what = section.get("what")
                age_s = section.get("age_s") or 0.0
                idle_s = section.get("idle_s") or 0.0
                kind = program.get("kind")
                if kind == "EvaluationService" and what != INVALIDATION:
                    es_idle = max(es_idle, idle_s)
                    if age_s > HALF_SECTION_S and idle_s >= HALF_ES_IDLE_S:
                        es_bad.append("%s %d s" % (what, age_s))
                if kind == "ScoringService" and not invalidating \
                        and age_s > HALF_SECTION_S:
                    ss_bad.append("%d s" % age_s)
                if kind == "ProxyService" and idle_s > HALF_SECTION_S:
                    ps_bad.append("%d s" % idle_s)

    without = sorted(label for label in probed
                     if not http.get(label) and not rpc.get(label))
    rows.append(("probe latencies for every probed program",
                 ", ".join(without) or "all", "all", not without))
    # The criteria of L1 and L2. A fault run (L3) is judged by the rows
    # above: F2 and F3 push these past half their trips by design, so
    # there they are only recorded.
    load_rows: list[Row] = []
    for name, table, timeout in (("favicon", http, HTTP_TIMEOUT_S),
                                 ("health()", rpc, RPC_TIMEOUT_S)):
        worst = max((p_quantile(values, 0.99) or 0.0
                     for values in table.values()), default=0.0)
        load_rows.append(("%s p99 under 1/3 of %g s" % (name, timeout),
                          "%.2f s" % worst, "%.2f s" % (timeout / 3),
                          worst < timeout / 3))
    load_rows.append(("at most 2 misses in 6", str(max_misses),
                      str(HALF_MISSES), max_misses <= HALF_MISSES))
    load_rows.append(("ES sections under half their trip",
                      ", ".join(es_bad[:3]) or "none", "none", not es_bad))
    load_rows.append(("SS executions under 90 s", ", ".join(ss_bad[:3])
                      or "none", "none", not ss_bad))
    load_rows.append(("PS sends idle under 90 s", ", ".join(ps_bad[:3])
                      or "none", "none", not ps_bad))
    if fault_run:
        load_rows = [(criterion, value, "(recorded: a fault run)", True)
                     for criterion, value, _, _ in load_rows]
    rows.extend(load_rows)
    rows.append(("longest ES idle_s in a section (margin)",
                 "%.1f s" % es_idle, "(recorded)", True))

    latency = ["| program | probe | p50 | p99 |", "|---|---|---|---|"]
    for name, table in (("favicon", http), ("health()", rpc)):
        for label in sorted(table):
            latency.append("| %s | %s | %.3f s | %.3f s |" % (
                label, name, p_quantile(table[label], 0.5),
                p_quantile(table[label], 0.99)))
    return rows, latency


def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    """Print the table of a run; return 1 if a criterion failed."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("run_dir")
    parser.add_argument("--cws", type=int, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv[1:])
    rows, latency = check(args.run_dir, args.cws, args.workers)
    print("| criterion | value | limit | result |")
    print("|---|---|---|---|")
    for criterion, value, limit, passed in rows:
        print("| %s | %s | %s | %s |" % (criterion, value, limit,
                                         "pass" if passed else "FAIL"))
    print()
    print("\n".join(latency))
    return 0 if all(row[3] for row in rows) else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))
```

  fi

  compose exec -d cms bash -c "echo \$\$ >/loadtest/run/watchdog.pid; exec cmsWatchdog --mode observe --process-source proc --status-history /loadtest/out/$NAME/watchdog_status.jsonl >>/loadtest/out/$NAME/watchdog.log 2>&1"
fi

  python3 "$HERE/watchdogstats.py" "$OUT" --cws "$CWS" --workers "$WORKERS" >"$OUT/watchdog.md" ||
    echo "A watchdog criterion failed, see $OUT/watchdog.md" >&2

fi

- [ ] **Step 5: Edit `cmstestsuite/loadtest/README.md`.** Add the flag to
  the options table (after the `--poll-cap` row):

```markdown
| `--watchdog` | off | also runs `cmsWatchdog` in observe mode with the `/proc` source in the `cms` container, and `watchdogstats.py` at the end (fork target only; see "Watchdog runs") |
```

  and, after the "Fault runs" section, add:

```markdown
## Watchdog runs

`run.sh --watchdog` (fork target only) runs
`cmsWatchdog --mode observe --process-source proc` in the `cms`
container from before ProxyService starts, with
`--status-history out/<name>/watchdog_status.jsonl` (one status per
cycle) and its output in `out/<name>/watchdog.log`. After the run,
`watchdogstats.py out/<name> --cws N --workers N` writes
`out/<name>/watchdog.md`: one row per criterion of "Harness L" in
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-watchdog-design.md`, `pass` or
`FAIL`, then the probe latencies (p50 and p99 per program):

- no `WOULD` line; holds and a red gate only inside the F2 and F3
  windows of `faults.json` (none without `--faults`);
- every program (ES, SS, PS, LogService, every CWS, AWS, every Worker)
  in every cycle from the driver's first request; ProxyService may
  appear late, since `run.sh` starts it once the contests exist;
- probe latencies for every probed program, favicon and `health()` p99
  under a third of their timeouts (5 s and 3 s);
- every near miss under half its trip: at most 2 misses in 6, no ES
  section older than 90 s with no statement for 30 s (re-evaluations
  aside), no SS execution over 90 s (unless it overlapped a
  re-evaluation), no PS send idle over 90 s; the longest ES `idle_s`
  inside a section is recorded as a margin.

With `--faults` too (L3), the criteria are those of L3: no `WOULD` line,
holds and a red gate only inside the F2 and F3 windows (so nothing for F1
and F4), every program in every cycle, and latency samples for every
probed program. The p99 and near-miss rows are then only recorded
(`(recorded: a fault run)`): F2 and F3 push them past half their trips by
design.
```

# Validation (controller-run, needs the user's permission)

These are not implementation tasks. The controller runs them after Task
21 and the final review, locally, and only once the user has allowed
them (as for #39). They need rootful Docker (`sg docker`, context
`default`: isolate works only there), and the steps marked **sudo**
need `sudo nsenter`: ask before each of those. Nothing here touches a
remote server. Record every result (pass or the exact deviation) for
the report on #38.

## V0: the whole suite and the docs (no permission needed)

```bash

export CMS_CONFIG=/home/areslolxd/.claude/jobs/6e6876f5/tmp/cms-watchdog.toml
GEVENT_SERVICE_FILES="cmstestsuite/unit_tests/service/twophase_evaluationservice_test.py cmstestsuite/unit_tests/service/twophase_reenqueue_test.py"
.venv/bin/pytest -p no:cacheprovider -q cmstestsuite/unit_tests/cmscontrib cmstestsuite/unit_tests/cmsranking \
    cmstestsuite/unit_tests/db/rankinggroup_test.py $GEVENT_SERVICE_FILES
.venv/bin/pytest -p no:cacheprovider -q docker/test_generate_config.py docker/test_status_sh.py
IGNORE_ARGS="--ignore=cmstestsuite/unit_tests/cmscontrib --ignore=cmstestsuite/unit_tests/cmsranking --ignore=cmstestsuite/unit_tests/db/rankinggroup_test.py"
for f in $GEVENT_SERVICE_FILES; do IGNORE_ARGS="$IGNORE_ARGS --ignore=$f"; done
.venv/bin/pytest -p no:cacheprovider -q cmstestsuite/unit_tests $IGNORE_ARGS
uvx -q pyflakes cms cmscommon cmscontrib cmsranking cmstaskenv cmstestsuite
bash /home/areslolxd/.claude/jobs/6e6876f5/tmp/w38-docs-check.sh
```

Pass: every group passes (the known pre-existing flakes aside, each
re-run once), pyflakes prints nothing, `docs check rc=0`.

## Rig P: the actuation drills D1-D15

**Setup** (once). In the worktree, write `.env` (git ignores it; never
commit it) and build and start the rig on rootful Docker:

```bash
cd /var/home/areslolxd/Documentos/cms/.worktrees/watchdog
export DOCKER_CONTEXT=default
rig() { sg docker -c "$(printf '%q ' "$@")"; }
dcp() { rig docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.prod.yml --env-file .env -p cmswdrig --profile localdb "$@"; }
PG_PW=$(openssl rand -hex 12)
cat >.env <<EOF
CMS_PROJECT_NAME=cmswdrig
CMS_USE_LOCALDB=true
POSTGRES_USER=cms
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=$PG_PW
POSTGRES_DB=cmsdb
CMS_DB_URL=postgresql+psycopg2://cms:$PG_PW@db:5432/cmsdb
CMS_SECRET_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 16)
CMS_ADMIN_USER=admin
CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -hex 12)
CMS_RWS_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -hex 12)
CMS_CONTEST_ID=ALL
CMS_CWS_COUNT=2
CMS_CWS_HTTP_PORT=18888
CMS_AWS_HTTP_PORT=18900
CMS_RWS_HTTP_PORT=18901
CMS_WORKER_COUNT=2
CMS_WATCHDOG=enforce
CMS_WATCHDOG_RESTART=web,core,infra,workers
EOF
dcp build cms ranking db-init
dcp up -d --wait
rig ./status.sh
```

Then, in the rig's Admin Web Server (`http://127.0.0.1:18900`, user
`admin`), create one contest with one Batch task (the sum of two
numbers, a correct C++ solution) and two users, and tick **Active**.
Submissions in the drills go through `cmsAddSubmission` in the `cms`
container (`dcp exec -T cms cmsAddSubmission --help`).

**P0, the image.** `dcp exec -T cms supervisord --version` prints
`4.2.5` (the version whose `getAllConfigInfo()` this plan checked), and
every CMS program of the status file has a kind:
`dcp exec -T cms python3 -c 'import json; s = json.load(open("/home/cmsuser/cms/run/watchdog.json")); print([(p["name"], p["kind"]) for p in s["programs"]])'`
shows `ContestWebServer`, `Worker`, ... for every `cms*` program and
`None` only for the loader or the bot.

**Caddy** (for D1-D3): run Caddy 2.11.7 with the profile of
`docs/docker-deployment.md` in front of the two shards:

```bash
cat >/tmp/w38-Caddyfile <<'EOF'
{
    admin localhost:12019
}
:18080 {
    reverse_proxy 127.0.0.1:18888 127.0.0.1:18889 {
        lb_policy cookie
        lb_try_duration 15s
        lb_try_interval 250ms
        health_uri /static/favicon.ico
        health_interval 10s
        health_timeout 5s
        health_fails 3
        health_passes 1
        fail_duration 10s
        max_fails 1
        transport http {
            dial_timeout 3s
            response_header_timeout 120s
        }
    }
}
EOF
rig docker run -d --name cmswdrig-caddy --network host -v /tmp/w38-Caddyfile:/etc/caddy/Caddyfile:ro caddy:2.11.7
curl -s localhost:12019/reverse_proxy/upstreams
```

Helpers used below:

```bash
spid() { dcp exec -T cms supervisorctl -c /home/cmsuser/cms/etc/supervisord.conf pid "$1"; }
sig() { dcp exec -T cms kill "-$1" "$(spid "$2")"; }      # sig STOP cmscontestwebserver1
evidence() { dcp exec -T cms ls -l /home/cmsuser/cms/log/watchdog; }
journal() { dcp exec -T cms cat /home/cmsuser/cms/log/watchdog/actions.jsonl; }
ledger() { dcp exec -T cms cat /home/cmsuser/cms/run/watchdog-ledger.json; }
```

Pass is read from `rig ./logs.sh --tail 300 cms`, `evidence`,
`journal`, `ledger` and `rig ./status.sh`. Between drills, wait for
`Resolved:` lines and let the budgets matter only where the drill says
so; restart the `cms` container (`dcp restart cms`) to reset the ledger
when a drill needs fresh budgets.

| Drill | Injection | Pass |
|---|---|---|
| D1 hung CWS | `sig STOP cmscontestwebserver1` | Caddy ejects it at about 35 s (`curl -s localhost:12019/reverse_proxy/upstreams`). At 65-90 s: an evidence file (State T, so `no USR1`, expected), TERM, KILL after 10 s, autorestart, a "answers again" line. Caddy readmits it. CWS 0 untouched. `ledger`: 1 real restart of it |
| D2 correlated web | `sig STOP cmscontestwebserver0; sig STOP cmscontestwebserver1`, 6 minutes, then `sig CONT` both | no restart; one H4 alert, repeated every 5 min; after CONT, `Resolved:` lines and no action |
| D3 hung AWS | `sig STOP cmsadminwebserver` | restarted; after recovery, the AWS **Overview** shows the line |
| D4 (a) | `rig docker pause cmswdrig-db-1` for 5 min; at its start `dcp exec cms supervisorctl -c /home/cmsuser/cms/etc/supervisord.conf restart cmsscoringservice`; then `rig docker unpause cmswdrig-db-1` | zero restarts while red; a red alert every 5 min; no restart decision for 120 s after green; SS cycles and never goes FATAL |
| D4 (b) | `rig docker stop cmswdrig-db-1` for at least 10 min, with the same SS restart at its start; then `rig docker start cmswdrig-db-1` | as (a), and SS goes FATAL (about 8 min of retries), then an R5 batch starts it about 30 s after green (`started cmsscoringservice, left FATAL ...`) |
| D5 lock holder | `dcp exec db psql -U cms -d cmsdb`, then `BEGIN; LOCK TABLE submission_results IN ACCESS EXCLUSIVE MODE;` (a), or `IN EXCLUSIVE MODE` (b); submit a solution; after the checks, `COMMIT;` | an alert headed by H3 that names the psql pid: in (a) even while 2 or more CWS fail (H4 listed after it); in (b) from ES's R3 trip alone, no CWS failing. No ES restart. After COMMIT, `Resolved:` and the submission is scored |
| D6 hung ES loop | `sig STOP cmsevaluationservice` | R2 restart; the startup sweep re-enqueues; the submission's score is right in the DB |
| D7 (a) **sudo** | set `CMS_DB_URL` with #39's kill-switch suffix `?connect_timeout=0&keepalives_idle=7200&keepalives_interval=75&keepalives_count=9&tcp_user_timeout=0`, `dcp up -d --wait`; submit a solution; while ES writes its results, read ES's client ports with `dcp exec -T db psql -U cms -d cmsdb -At -c "SELECT client_port FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE application_name = 'cmsEvaluationService 0'"` and run `COMPOSE_PROFILES=localdb python3 cmstestsuite/loadtest/faults.py --project cmswdrig --compose-file docker/docker-compose.prod.yml --env-file .env --out /tmp/w38-d7.json --drop-client-ports <ports, comma-separated> --max-s 300`; Ctrl-C it right after the restart is confirmed | R3 trips at about 180 s with no statements: a restart, and grading resumes. An H3 alert may then mark a blocker `orphan of pid N` until it ends; end it with the runbook's step ("A lock blocks CMS") |
| D7 (b) **sudo** | the same with #39's defaults (no suffix) | the write fails at about 60 s, the lock is released, nothing trips |
| D8 long re-evaluation | on the stack that L2 leaves (harness, observe mode), re-evaluate a whole contest in its AWS | no WOULD line; `re-evaluation still running` if it passes 180 s. Record its duration and the longest ES `idle_s` inside a section (`watchdog.md` of a re-run of `watchdogstats.py`) as margins |
| D9 hung ranking | `dcp stop ranking`; `rig docker run -d --rm --name cmswdrig-fakerws --network cmswdrig_default --network-alias ranking python:3.12-slim python3 -c 'import socket; s = socket.create_server(("0.0.0.0", 8890)); c = [s.accept() for _ in iter(int, 1)]'`; after the checks, `rig docker stop cmswdrig-fakerws; dcp start ranking` | PS never trips (each request ends within 125 s, under the 180 s idle trip); a `ranking ... does not answer` alert |
| D10 stuck Worker | submit a solution, and while a Worker evaluates it, `sig STOP cmsworker0`; repeat with `CMS_WATCHDOG_RESTART=web,core,infra` | ES disables it at 600 s or more; 180 s later: killpg, autorestart, reconnect, re-enabled; `dcp exec -T cms ps -eo pid,pgid,args \| grep [i]solate` prints nothing, and the next submission evaluates on that box. Without `workers`: an alert only (`WOULD restart`, H9) |
| D11 budget | `sig STOP cmscontestwebserver1` again after each restart; then, within the same hour, `sig STOP cmsadminwebserver` and later `sig STOP cmsscoringservice` | exactly 2 restarts of CWS 1, then `will not restart ... before HH:MM UTC` every 5 min; AWS is restarted (pool 3/3), SS gets an H7 hold |
| D12 pauses | (a) `sig STOP cmswatchdog` for 45 s, then `sig CONT cmswatchdog`; (b) the same for 5 min; (c) `rig docker pause cmswdrig-cms-1` for 120 s, then unpause | (a) `paused for 45 s`, windows cleared, no action. (b) `rig ./status.sh` shows a stale watchdog line; on resume in (b) and (c): the self-guard dumps the watchdog's stack and exits, supervisord restarts it, and after its 120 s grace there is no action |
| D13 operator actions | `dcp up -d --force-recreate cms`; `supervisorctl stop` of `cmscontestwebserver1` and of `cmsworker1`; `supervisorctl stop cmswatchdog` | no action within the grace; STOPPED programs are never started or counted; no actions while the watchdog is stopped |
| D14 watchdog dies mid-restart | `sig STOP cmscontestwebserver1`; watch `journal`, and as soon as the booking line appears, `dcp exec -T cms kill -9 "$(spid cmswatchdog)"`; three times in a row | nothing is left STOPPED; each new watchdog keeps the ledger and re-detects after its grace; `ledger` shows 2 restarts, then an H7 hold |
| D15 SS stuck on a lost connection **sudo** | with the kill-switch suffix, while a submission is being scored, drop only SS's connections (`application_name = 'cmsScoringService 0'`) with `faults.py --drop-client-ports` as in D7 | SS trips R3 at about 180 s with no lock wait, is restarted, and scoring resumes |

**Teardown:** `dcp down -v; rig docker rm -f cmswdrig-caddy; rm .env`.

## Harness L: false positives under load

From `cmstestsuite/loadtest/` (rootful Docker through `run.sh`'s own
`sg docker`); `./build.sh fork feat/watchdog` first. After each run,
`./teardown.sh fork` (but keep the L2 stack for D8 first).

| Run | Command | Pass (`out/<name>/watchdog.md`) |
|---|---|---|
| L1 | `./run.sh --target fork --profile full --name w38-l1 --watchdog` (the `full1` defaults: 250 users) | every row `pass`: zero WOULD lines and zero holds; the gate always green; favicon and `health()` p99 under 1/3 of their timeouts; every near-miss maximum under half its trip; every program in every cycle |
| L2 | `./run.sh --target fork --profile full --name w38-l2 --users-a 700 --users-b 300 --watchdog`, with the Worker and CWS counts of the README's earlier 1000-user runs | the same; record the longest ES `idle_s` inside a section as a margin |
| L3 **sudo** | `./run.sh --target fork --profile full --name w38-l3 --contest 2100 --faults --watchdog` | the gate red only inside the F2 and F3 windows; holds only there; zero WOULD lines; nothing for F1 and F4 |

## Game day on production

The operator runs it, not the controller, from "The game day" in
`docs/contest-day.md`: in a quiet window, once in observe mode and once
right after enforce is switched on (spec, Rollout).
